# Web Client Core Implementation Plan (Plan 6 of 11)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The web client core: design tokens and base components (`packages/ui`), shared views and view-models over a `DataSource` interface (`packages/app-core`), the online HTTP + WebSocket DataSource with a local rule-engine pre-check, and the Next.js 15 shell (`apps/web`) with sign-in (all four methods), workspace/project navigation, invite acceptance, Board, List, My Work, the attention strip, the ticket sheet, and the reopen-option dialog.

**Architecture:** Views live in `packages/app-core` and consume only the `DataSource` interface (coordination amendment 10) — implemented here as the online HTTP+WS source and again by Plan 10 as the replica source; views never import HTTP code, and a scanner test proves it. Colour originates in `packages/ui/src/tokens.ts` alone (amendment 11), also enforced by a scanner test. Every honesty guarantee the UI makes — provenance never anonymous, restricted children never hidden, grouping never altering counts, both clocks in history, server refusals shown verbatim — is proven against a planted defect before it is trusted (spec honesty contract, invariant 6).

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 5.6, React 19, Next.js 15 (App Router), better-auth client, Vitest 3 + Testing Library (jsdom), Playwright smoke; the pnpm/turbo monorepo from Plan 1.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-20-gisik-nokma-product-design.md` (§7 views, §1 honesty contract, §5 web-is-online-only, §6 visibility) — read alongside `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-20-00-series-coordination.md`, the series authority. Where a Plan 1–4 detail disagrees with the coordination document or with the pinned surface below, coordination wins.

## Global Constraints

- Package scope is `@gisik-nokma/*` (coordination §1); workspace globs `packages/*` and `apps/*`; Node `>=22.0.0 <23`; `pnpm@9.15.0`; turbo scripts `build` / `test` / `typecheck` (Plan 1 Task 1).
- `reference/` is frozen — never edit anything under it. Cite it; do not touch it.
- `Features.md` §8 and `test-cases/08-web-client.md` (cases 8.x only — no other numbers) move in the same change set as the work (Task 16).
- **No colour literal outside `packages/ui/src/tokens.ts` in any client package** (coordination amendment 11 — the base's token discipline: its viewer "holds no data and no colour of its own", `reference/convenience-corner-tracker/README.md`). Enforced by a test (Task 2).
- **Views never import HTTP code** (amendment 10). Enforced by a test (Task 7).
- Provenance floor (honesty contract 1): the UI never launders a claim into an anonymous fact. "Done" always unfolds to "marked Done by X on date".
- Every honesty guarantee is proven against a planted defect before it is trusted (honesty contract 6): render the deceptive scenario, assert the honest output.
- The web client is online-only (spec §5): every action round-trips; the local rule engine gives instant feedback only — the server remains the authority, and its refusals render verbatim.
- Desktop-responsive only. Mobile-first layouts belong to Plan 10's apps (amendment 17); the web shell targets desktop widths and degrades gracefully to ~960px.
- ESM throughout: `"type": "module"`, NodeNext resolution, explicit `.js` suffixes on relative imports (Plan 1's convention).
- CODING_STANDARDS (`/Users/kiro/my_projects/CODING_STANDARDS.md`): BEM (`block__element--modifier`) for CSS class names; intent-revealing identifiers; DIP — inject `fetch`, WebSocket factories, clocks, and randomness; testing pyramid (unit-heavy, one Playwright smoke at the top).
- Commits are prose that says what was found and why it matters (constraint carried from the base). One PR per change, branched off `main`, merged only with explicit approval.

## Consumed surface (pinned — do not re-derive)

Wire types, verbatim from the series contract (`@gisik-nokma/domain`, Plan 1):

```ts
type EventEnvelope = {
  id: string;                                  // client-generated ULID
  workspaceId: string;
  entity: { kind: 'workspace'|'project'|'ticket'|'workflow'|'ticket-type'|'membership'; id: string };
  type: string; version: number; payload: unknown;
  actorId: string; origin: 'web'|'mobile'|'desktop'|'server';
  clientTime: string;                          // the user's claim about when
  serverTime: string;                          // the system's fact about when
  seq: number;                                 // per-entity, server-assigned
};
type SubmittedEvent = Omit<EventEnvelope, 'serverTime' | 'seq'>;

type IngestResult =
  | { status: 'committed'; eventId: string; seq: number; serverTime: string }
  | { status: 'invalid'; eventId: string | null; code: string; message: string }
  | { status: 'refused'; eventId: string; rule: string; reason: string };
```

Per coordination, the client-facing `IngestResult` type is imported from `@gisik-nokma/domain` (clients never import server code); `apps/server`'s ingest service produces it. Task 5 guards the export (and flags the Plan 1 definition site in Open questions).

Server surface this plan consumes (Plan 5 + earlier; paths verbatim):

- `POST /v1/events` body `{ "events": [ … ] }` (1–100), → `{ "results": [ <IngestResult>, … ] }` index-aligned (Plan 1).
- WS `/v1/stream` (Plan 5, amendment 13): client → `{type:'subscribe', workspaceId, afterSeq}`; server → `{type:'event', envelope}` · `{type:'purge', entityId}` · `{type:'caught-up', seq}` · ping/pong.
- `GET /v1/projects/:id/board` · `GET /v1/projects/:id/tickets` (FilterSpec + grouping) · `GET /v1/search` · `GET /v1/me/work` · `GET /v1/workspaces/:id/attention` · `GET`/`PUT /v1/me/preferences` (amendment 14) — Plan 5.
- `GET /v1/tickets/:id/history` → `{ ticketId, events }`, each event `{id, type, version, payload, actorId, origin, clientTime, serverTime, seq}` — Plan 3, wrapped by Plan 4's read enforcement.
- `GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/tickets/:ticketId` → `{ ticket, parent, rollup }` with `parent: null | {kind:'ticket', id, title} | {kind:'restricted', id}` — Plan 4. **Note the `/api/` prefix; consume verbatim, do not normalise to `/v1/`.**
- Auth `/api/auth/*` — better-auth with `phoneNumber`, `magicLink`, `passkey` plugins and the Google social provider (Plan 4). No passwords.

Domain/rule-engine surface:

- `@gisik-nokma/rule-engine`: `evaluateEvent(env: EventEnvelope, ctx: EngineContext): Promise<Accept | Refusal>` (frozen signature); `EngineContext = { getEntityState(kind, id); getChildrenStates(ticketId); getWorkflowDef(workflowId, version?) }`; `Refusal = { refused: true; rule: string; reason: string }`; `REFUSAL_RULES` (Plan 2) including `restrict-to`, `require-comment`, `require-evidence`, `require-field`, `require-different-user`, `illegal-transition`, `parent-terminal-with-open-children`.
- `TicketState` per coordination §4: `{ kind:'ticket', id, workspaceId, projectId, typeId, parentId, workflowId, stateId, stateCategory, assigneeId, collaboratorIds, fields: Record<string, FieldClaim>, transitions: {toStateId, actorId, seq}[] }`; `FieldClaim = { value, actorId, clientTime, serverTime }` (Plan 3).
- `WorkflowDef` with `initialStateId` (coordination §3) and optional `blocked?: boolean` per state (amendment 9); states carry `{id, name, category: 'open'|'active'|'terminal'}`; transitions `{from: stateId | '*', to, rules: Rule[]}` (Plan 2).
- Owner decision (coordination): `ticket.created`/`ticket.reparented` under a terminal parent refuse as rule **`hierarchy.parent-terminal`**; the client offers reopen; on acceptance the parent's `ticket.transitioned` carries `cause: { kind: 'reopened-for-child'; childTicketId }` and is emitted **before** the child event; the parent workflow's own transition rules still apply to the reopen.
- Rollups to the viewer: `{ totalChildren, terminalChildren, restrictedCount }` (prompt-pinned spelling — the client contract; the online mapper adapts whatever field spelling the server ships). Restricted parent placeholder: `{ kind: 'restricted' }`.
- `docToPlainText(doc: unknown): string` from `@gisik-nokma/domain` (amendment 12; Task 5 guards it).

**Adaptation rule (stated once, applies everywhere):** Plan 5 is written in parallel; the exact JSON spellings of its read responses may drift from the mapper code below. The ONLY place allowed to adapt is `packages/app-core/src/online/` (mappers and paths). The `DataSource` signatures and view types in this plan are the client contract and do not move; Plan 10's replica implements the same signatures. Reads with no pinned endpoint (workspace directory, members, workflow-def read, invites) are each isolated to one adapter function and named in Open questions.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tokens test**

The base proved its palette instead of trusting it — "Every declared token contrast pair meets its minimum" was covered by a tool (`reference/convenience-corner-tracker/tools/test_build_tracker.py`, base test case 1.1), and roles were derived from primitives, never authored twice. Same discipline here.

`packages/ui/test/tokens.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  CONTRAST_PAIRS,
  TOKEN_ROLES,
  colorOf,
  cssVariableName,
  primitives,
  themeStylesheet,
  themes,
  type ThemeName,
} from '../src/tokens.js';

const THEME_NAMES: ThemeName[] = ['light', 'dark'];

/** WCAG relative luminance from a #rrggbb (or #rrggbbaa — alpha ignored) hex literal. */
function relativeLuminance(hex: string): number {
  const channels = [hex.slice(1, 3), hex.slice(3, 5), hex.slice(5, 7)].map((pair) => {
    const srgb = Number.parseInt(pair, 16) / 255;
    return srgb <= 0.04045 ? srgb / 12.92 : ((srgb + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
  });
  return 0.2126 * channels[0] + 0.7152 * channels[1] + 0.0722 * channels[2];
}

function contrastRatio(colorA: string, colorB: string): number {
  const [brighter, darker] = [relativeLuminance(colorA), relativeLuminance(colorB)].sort(
    (first, second) => second - first,
  );
  return (brighter + 0.05) / (darker + 0.05);
}

describe('token structure', () => {
  it('every primitive is a hex literal', () => {
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(primitives)) {
      expect(value, `primitive ${name}`).toMatch(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}([0-9a-f]{2})?$/);
    }
  });

  it('every role in both themes resolves to a declared primitive', () => {
    for (const theme of THEME_NAMES) {
      for (const role of TOKEN_ROLES) {
        const primitiveName = themes[theme][role];
        expect(primitives[primitiveName], `${theme}.${role}`).toBeDefined();
        expect(colorOf(theme, role)).toBe(primitives[primitiveName]);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe('declared contrast pairs meet their minimums (the base test case 1.1, reborn)', () => {
  for (const theme of THEME_NAMES) {
    for (const pair of CONTRAST_PAIRS) {
      it(`${theme}: ${pair.fg} on ${pair.bg} ≥ ${pair.minimum}`, () => {
        const ratio = contrastRatio(colorOf(theme, pair.fg), colorOf(theme, pair.bg));
        expect(ratio).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(pair.minimum);
      });
    }
  }
});

describe('CSS variable emission', () => {
  it('kebab-cases role names under the --gn prefix', () => {
    expect(cssVariableName('surface')).toBe('--gn-surface');
    expect(cssVariableName('surfaceRaised')).toBe('--gn-surface-raised');
    expect(cssVariableName('categoryTerminal')).toBe('--gn-category-terminal');
  });

  it('ships light as the default, dark by explicit choice, dark by OS preference when unchosen', () => {
    const stylesheet = themeStylesheet();
    expect(stylesheet).toContain(`:root { --gn-surface: ${colorOf('light', 'surface')};`);
    expect(stylesheet).toContain(`[data-theme="dark"] { --gn-surface: ${colorOf('dark', 'surface')};`);
    expect(stylesheet).toContain('@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
    expect(stylesheet).toContain(':root:not([data-theme="light"])');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/tokens.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * The one colour origin for every Gisik Nokma client (coordination amendment 11).
 * The base made this discipline literal: its viewer "holds no data and no colour
 * of its own" — every colour came from design-system/tokens.json as roles
 * resolved from primitives (reference/convenience-corner-tracker/README.md).
 * This file carries that forward: primitives are the palette, roles are the
 * meaning, and no colour literal is legal anywhere else in any client package
 * (enforced by test/token-discipline.test.ts).
 */

export type ThemeName = 'light' | 'dark';

export const primitives = {
  white: '#ffffff',
  paper: '#f7f6f3',
  sand: '#edebe6',
  ink: '#1c1b18',
  inkMuted: '#5f5c55',
  line: '#d9d6cf',
  lineStrong: '#a8a49a',
  blue600: '#2a5fd0',
  blue300: '#7aa2ff',
  navy900: '#101625',
  red600: '#b3261e',
  red300: '#ff8a80',
  redInk: '#300a06',
  amber700: '#8a5a00',
  amber300: '#e0a94f',
  amberInk: '#241a00',
  green700: '#1e7a34',
  green300: '#62c07a',
  greenInk: '#05230e',
  violet600: '#6b5ca8',
  violet300: '#b3a6e3',
  magenta600: '#a8326e',
  magenta300: '#e88bb8',
  coal: '#1b1a17',
  coalRaised: '#242320',
  coalSunken: '#121110',
  fog: '#ece9e2',
  fogMuted: '#a7a399',
  seam: '#3a3833',
  seamStrong: '#57544c',
  scrimLight: '#1c1b1866',
  scrimDark: '#000000a6',
} as const;

export type PrimitiveName = keyof typeof primitives;

export const TOKEN_ROLES = [
  'surface',
  'surfaceRaised',
  'surfaceSunken',
  'surfaceOverlay',
  'text',
  'textMuted',
  'textInverted',
  'border',
  'borderStrong',
  'focusRing',
  'shadow',
  'accent',
  'accentText',
  'danger',
  'dangerText',
  'warning',
  'warningText',
  'success',
  'successText',
  'categoryOpen',
  'categoryActive',
  'categoryTerminal',
  'blocked',
  'pending',
  'restricted',
  'contested',
] as const;

export type TokenRole = (typeof TOKEN_ROLES)[number];

export const themes: Record<ThemeName, Record<TokenRole, PrimitiveName>> = {
  light: {
    surface: 'paper',
    surfaceRaised: 'white',
    surfaceSunken: 'sand',
    surfaceOverlay: 'scrimLight',
    text: 'ink',
    textMuted: 'inkMuted',
    textInverted: 'white',
    border: 'line',
    borderStrong: 'lineStrong',
    focusRing: 'blue600',
    shadow: 'scrimLight',
    accent: 'blue600',
    accentText: 'white',
    danger: 'red600',
    dangerText: 'white',
    warning: 'amber700',
    warningText: 'white',
    success: 'green700',
    successText: 'white',
    categoryOpen: 'inkMuted',
    categoryActive: 'blue600',
    categoryTerminal: 'green700',
    blocked: 'red600',
    pending: 'amber700',
    restricted: 'violet600',
    contested: 'magenta600',
  },
  dark: {
    surface: 'coal',
    surfaceRaised: 'coalRaised',
    surfaceSunken: 'coalSunken',
    surfaceOverlay: 'scrimDark',
    text: 'fog',
    textMuted: 'fogMuted',
    textInverted: 'ink',
    border: 'seam',
    borderStrong: 'seamStrong',
    focusRing: 'blue300',
    shadow: 'scrimDark',
    accent: 'blue300',
    accentText: 'navy900',
    danger: 'red300',
    dangerText: 'redInk',
    warning: 'amber300',
    warningText: 'amberInk',
    success: 'green300',
    successText: 'greenInk',
    categoryOpen: 'fogMuted',
    categoryActive: 'blue300',
    categoryTerminal: 'green300',
    blocked: 'red300',
    pending: 'amber300',
    restricted: 'violet300',
    contested: 'magenta300',
  },
};

/**
 * Contrast guarantees the origin declares about itself. The tokens test
 * verifies each pair at its minimum in BOTH themes — a palette edit that
 * breaks readability fails the build, it does not ship.
 */
export const CONTRAST_PAIRS: ReadonlyArray<{ fg: TokenRole; bg: TokenRole; minimum: number }> = [
  { fg: 'text', bg: 'surface', minimum: 4.5 },
  { fg: 'text', bg: 'surfaceRaised', minimum: 4.5 },
  { fg: 'textMuted', bg: 'surface', minimum: 4.5 },
  { fg: 'accentText', bg: 'accent', minimum: 4.5 },
  { fg: 'dangerText', bg: 'danger', minimum: 4.5 },
  { fg: 'warningText', bg: 'warning', minimum: 4.5 },
  { fg: 'successText', bg: 'success', minimum: 4.5 },
];

export function colorOf(theme: ThemeName, role: TokenRole): string {
  return primitives[themes[theme][role]];
}

/** `surfaceRaised` → `--gn-surface-raised`. */
export function cssVariableName(role: TokenRole): string {
  return `--gn-${role.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (upper) => `-${upper.toLowerCase()}`)}`;
}

function declarationsFor(theme: ThemeName): string {
  return TOKEN_ROLES.map((role) => `${cssVariableName(role)}: ${colorOf(theme, role)};`).join(' ');
}

/**
 * The stylesheet the shells inline. Light is the default; dark applies via an
 * explicit `data-theme="dark"` on the root element, or via OS preference when
 * no explicit choice was made. Both themes ship — switching never reloads.
 */
export function themeStylesheet(): string {
  return [
    `:root { ${declarationsFor('light')} }`,
    `[data-theme="dark"] { ${declarationsFor('dark')} }`,
    `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root:not([data-theme="light"]) { ${declarationsFor('dark')} } }`,
  ].join('\n');
}
```

`packages/ui/src/index.ts` (components join in Task 3):

```ts
export {
  CONTRAST_PAIRS,
  TOKEN_ROLES,
  colorOf,
  cssVariableName,
  primitives,
  themeStylesheet,
  themes,
  type PrimitiveName,
  type ThemeName,
  type TokenRole,
} from './tokens.js';
```

- Test: `packages/ui/test/token-discipline.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the repo layout (client source trees under `packages/*/src`, `apps/web`).
- Produces: `findColourLiterals(content: string): string[]`, `scanTreeForColourLiterals(root: string): ColourHit[]`, `existingClientSourceRoots(): string[]` — Plans 7/8/10 add their client packages to `CLIENT_SOURCE_ROOTS` (the list already names them, so growth is automatic).

**Why this shape:** the base's viewer holds "no colour of its own" and a tool proved colours were derived, not authored (`reference/convenience-corner-tracker/`, README and `test_colours_are_derived_from_tokens_json_not_authored`). Amendment 11 makes that a series rule. A rule without a planted-defect proof is a hope, so the scanner is tested against defects before the clean-tree assertion is trusted.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`packages/ui/test/token-discipline.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  REPO_ROOT,
  existingClientSourceRoots,
  findColourLiterals,
  scanTreeForColourLiterals,
} from './helpers/colour-scan.js';

describe('the scanner itself is proven against planted defects before it is trusted', () => {
  it('catches hex (3–8 digit), rgb(), rgba(), hsl(), oklch() and color-mix()', () => {
    expect(findColourLiterals('color: #fff;')).toEqual(['#fff']);
    expect(findColourLiterals('background: #b3261e;')).toEqual(['#b3261e']);
    expect(findColourLiterals("const scrim = '#1c1b1866';")).toEqual(['#1c1b1866']);
    expect(findColourLiterals('border: 1px solid rgb(0, 0, 0);')).toEqual(['rgb(']);
    expect(findColourLiterals('color: rgba(0,0,0,.5)')).toEqual(['rgba(']);
    expect(findColourLiterals('color: hsl(9 80% 40%)')).toEqual(['hsl(']);
    expect(findColourLiterals('color: oklch(0.7 0.1 250)')).toEqual(['oklch(']);
    expect(findColourLiterals('color: color-mix(in srgb, red, blue)')).toEqual(['color-mix(']);
  });

  it('lets colour-free source through', () => {
    expect(findColourLiterals("import { colorOf } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';")).toEqual([]);
    expect(findColourLiterals('color: var(--gn-text);')).toEqual([]);
    expect(findColourLiterals("const ULID = '01J9GTKA000000000000000000';")).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('token discipline (coordination amendment 11)', () => {
  it('finds no colour literal outside packages/ui/src/tokens.ts across client source trees', () => {
    const hits = existingClientSourceRoots().flatMap((root) => scanTreeForColourLiterals(root));
    const report = hits.map((hit) => `${hit.file}:${hit.line} ${hit.match}`).join('\n');
    expect(hits, `colour literals outside the origin:\n${report}`).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('tokens.ts itself is exempt because it is the origin — and it does hold colours', () => {
    const origin = readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, 'packages/ui/src/tokens.ts'), 'utf8');
    expect(findColourLiterals(origin).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});
```

Expected: FAIL — `Cannot find module './helpers/colour-scan.js'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the scanner**

`packages/ui/test/helpers/colour-scan.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

export type ColourHit = { file: string; line: number; match: string };

/** packages/ui/test/helpers → four levels up is the repo root. */
export const REPO_ROOT = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../..', import.meta.url));

/**
 * Every client source tree, present or future. Trees that do not exist yet are
 * skipped; the moment a plan creates one, the discipline covers it with no
 * edit here. apps/server is deliberately absent — it renders nothing.
 */
export const CLIENT_SOURCE_ROOTS = [
  'packages/ui/src',
  'packages/app-core/src',
  'packages/editor/src',
  'apps/web/src',
  'apps/web/app',
  'apps/desktop/src',
  'apps/mobile/src',
];

const COLOUR_LITERAL = /#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\b|\b(?:rgb|rgba|hsl|hsla|oklch|oklab|color-mix)\s*\(/g;

const SCANNED_EXTENSIONS = ['.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.css'];
const SKIPPED_DIRECTORIES = new Set(['node_modules', 'dist', '.next', 'coverage', 'test-results']);

/** The one legal colour origin (coordination amendment 11). */
const ORIGIN_SUFFIX = join('packages', 'ui', 'src', 'tokens.ts');

export function findColourLiterals(content: string): string[] {
  return content.match(COLOUR_LITERAL) ?? [];
}

export function existingClientSourceRoots(): string[] {
  return CLIENT_SOURCE_ROOTS.map((root) => join(REPO_ROOT, root)).filter((root) => existsSync(root));
}

export function scanTreeForColourLiterals(root: string): ColourHit[] {
  const hits: ColourHit[] = [];
  walk(root, root, hits);
  return hits;
}

function walk(directory: string, root: string, hits: ColourHit[]): void {
  for (const entry of readdirSync(directory)) {
    const path = join(directory, entry);
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) {
      if (!SKIPPED_DIRECTORIES.has(entry)) walk(path, root, hits);
      continue;
    }
    if (!SCANNED_EXTENSIONS.some((extension) => entry.endsWith(extension))) continue;
    if (path.endsWith(ORIGIN_SUFFIX)) continue;
    const lines = readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split('\n');
    lines.forEach((line, index) => {
      for (const match of findColourLiterals(line)) {
        hits.push({ file: relative(root, path), line: index + 1, match });
      }
    });
  }
}
```

### Task 3: Base components — Button, Chip, Card, Sheet, Dialog, Table, Avatar

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ui/src/ui.css`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/button.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/chip.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/avatar.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/dialog.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/sheet.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/components/table.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/index.ts` (re-export components)
- Test: `packages/ui/test/components.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: token CSS variables (`--gn-*`, Task 1); the discipline scanner now watches these files (Task 2).
- Produces (every client view builds from these — exact names):
  - `Button` / `ButtonProps` (`variant?: 'primary' | 'ghost' | 'danger'`, plus native button props)
  - `Chip` / `ChipProps` / `ChipTone = 'neutral' | 'open' | 'active' | 'terminal' | 'blocked' | 'pending' | 'restricted' | 'contested'` — tone dot uses the matching token role, so state-category colours are consistent everywhere
  - `Card` / `CardProps` (`children`, `className?`) — a plain container, never itself a button (cards hold interactive children)
  - `Avatar` / `AvatarProps` (`name: string; size?: 'small' | 'medium'`) and `initialsOf(name: string): string`
  - `Dialog` / `DialogProps` (`open`, `title`, `onClose`, `children`) — `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, Escape closes, scrim click closes
  - `Sheet` / `SheetProps` (`open`, `title`, `onClose`, `children`) — right-side panel, same a11y contract
  - `Table<Row>` / `TableProps<Row>` / `TableColumn<Row> = { id: string; header: ReactNode; cell: (row: Row) => ReactNode }` — horizontal scroll lives inside the table wrapper, never the page
- CSS class names are BEM (`gn-button`, `gn-board-card__title`, `gn-chip--blocked`) per CODING_STANDARDS.

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Avatar, initialsOf } from '../src/components/avatar.js';

describe('Button', () => {
  it('defaults to the ghost variant and carries explicit variants as BEM modifiers', () => {
    render(
      <>
        <Button>Plain</Button>
        <Button variant="primary">Go</Button>
        <Button variant="danger">Discard</Button>
      </>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plain' })).toHaveClass('gn-button--ghost');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Go' })).toHaveClass('gn-button--primary');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard' })).toHaveClass('gn-button--danger');
  });

  it('is type=button by default so forms are never submitted by accident', () => {

describe('Chip', () => {

describe('Avatar', () => {

    expect(initialsOf('Anselma Marak')).toBe('AM');
    expect(initialsOf('Kiro')).toBe('KI');

    render(<Avatar name="Anselma Marak" />);
```

`packages/ui/src/components/button.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes } from 'react';

export type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'ghost' | 'danger';
export type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: ButtonVariant };

export function Button({ variant = 'ghost', className, type, ...rest }: ButtonProps) {
  const classes = ['gn-button', `gn-button--${variant}`, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  return <button {...rest} type={type ?? 'button'} className={classes} />;
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/chip.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

export type ChipTone =
  | 'neutral'
  | 'open'
  | 'active'
  | 'terminal'
  | 'blocked'
  | 'pending'
  | 'restricted'
  | 'contested';

export type ChipProps = { tone?: ChipTone; children: ReactNode; title?: string };

export function Chip({ tone = 'neutral', children, title }: ChipProps) {
  return (
    <span className={`gn-chip gn-chip--${tone}`} title={title}>
      <span className="gn-chip__dot" aria-hidden="true" />
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

export type CardProps = { children: ReactNode; className?: string };

/** A plain raised container. Never itself interactive — cards hold buttons. */
export function Card({ children, className }: CardProps) {
  return <div className={['gn-card', className].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}>{children}</div>;
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/avatar.tsx`:

```tsx
export type AvatarProps = { name: string; size?: 'small' | 'medium' };

export function initialsOf(name: string): string {
  const words = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return '?';
  if (words.length === 1) return words[0].slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  return (words[0][0] + words[words.length - 1][0]).toUpperCase();
}

export function Avatar({ name, size = 'medium' }: AvatarProps) {
  return (
    <span className={`gn-avatar gn-avatar--${size}`} title={name} aria-label={name}>
      {initialsOf(name)}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';

export type DialogProps = { open: boolean; title: string; onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode };

export function Dialog({ open, title, onClose, children }: DialogProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div className="gn-dialog__scrim" data-testid="dialog-scrim" onClick={onClose}>
      <section
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        className="gn-dialog"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <header className="gn-dialog__header">
          <h2 className="gn-dialog__title">{title}</h2>
          <button type="button" className="gn-dialog__close" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}>
            ×
          </button>
        </header>
        <div className="gn-dialog__body">{children}</div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';

export type SheetProps = { open: boolean; title: string; onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode };

/** A right-hand side panel with the same accessibility contract as Dialog. */
export function Sheet({ open, title, onClose, children }: SheetProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div className="gn-sheet__scrim" data-testid="sheet-scrim" onClick={onClose}>
      <section
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        className="gn-sheet"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <header className="gn-sheet__header">
          <h2 className="gn-sheet__title">{title}</h2>
          <button type="button" className="gn-sheet__close" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}>
            ×
          </button>
        </header>
        <div className="gn-sheet__body">{children}</div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/table.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

export type TableColumn<Row> = { id: string; header: ReactNode; cell: (row: Row) => ReactNode };

export type TableProps<Row> = {
  columns: TableColumn<Row>[];
  rows: Row[];
  rowKey: (row: Row) => string;
  onRowClick?: (row: Row) => void;
  caption?: string;
};

export function Table<Row>({ columns, rows, rowKey, onRowClick, caption }: TableProps<Row>) {
  return (
    <div className="gn-table__scroll">
      <table className="gn-table">
        {caption ? <caption className="gn-table__caption">{caption}</caption> : null}
        <thead>
          <tr>
            {columns.map((column) => (
              <th key={column.id} scope="col" className="gn-table__header">
                {column.header}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <tr
              key={rowKey(row)}
              className={onRowClick ? 'gn-table__row gn-table__row--clickable' : 'gn-table__row'}
              onClick={onRowClick ? () => onRowClick(row) : undefined}
            >
              {columns.map((column) => (
                <td key={column.id} className="gn-table__cell">
                  {column.cell(row)}
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
/* @gisik-nokma/ui — component styles. BEM classes; every colour is a token
   variable from tokens.ts (the one origin). No literal colours here: the
   token-discipline test scans this file. */

.gn-button {
  font: inherit;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--gn-border-strong);
  background: var(--gn-surface-raised);
  color: var(--gn-text);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.gn-button:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--gn-focus-ring);
  outline-offset: 1px;
}
.gn-button--primary {
  background: var(--gn-accent);
  border-color: var(--gn-accent);
  color: var(--gn-accent-text);
}
.gn-button--danger {
  background: var(--gn-danger);
  border-color: var(--gn-danger);
  color: var(--gn-danger-text);
}
.gn-button:disabled {
  opacity: 0.55;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.gn-chip {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--gn-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 0.1rem 0.6rem;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  background: var(--gn-surface-raised);
  color: var(--gn-text);
}
.gn-chip__dot {
  width: 0.55rem;
  height: 0.55rem;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--gn-text-muted);
}
.gn-chip--open .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-category-open); }
.gn-chip--active .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-category-active); }
.gn-chip--terminal .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-category-terminal); }
.gn-chip--blocked .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-blocked); }
.gn-chip--pending .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-pending); }
.gn-chip--restricted .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-restricted); }
.gn-chip--contested .gn-chip__dot { background: var(--gn-contested); }

.gn-card {
  background: var(--gn-surface-raised);
  border: 1px solid var(--gn-border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 0.75rem;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px var(--gn-shadow);
}

.gn-avatar {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--gn-surface-sunken);
  color: var(--gn-text);
  border: 1px solid var(--gn-border);
  font-weight: 600;
}
.gn-avatar--medium { width: 2rem; height: 2rem; font-size: 0.8rem; }
.gn-avatar--small { width: 1.5rem; height: 1.5rem; font-size: 0.65rem; }

.gn-dialog__scrim,
.gn-sheet__scrim {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  background: var(--gn-surface-overlay);
  display: flex;
  z-index: 40;
}
.gn-dialog__scrim { align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
.gn-sheet__scrim { justify-content: flex-end; }

.gn-dialog,
.gn-sheet {
  background: var(--gn-surface-raised);
  color: var(--gn-text);
  border: 1px solid var(--gn-border);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px var(--gn-shadow);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  max-height: 90vh;
}
.gn-dialog {
  border-radius: 10px;
  width: min(34rem, 92vw);
}
.gn-sheet {
  width: min(40rem, 94vw);

}
.gn-dialog__header,
.gn-sheet__header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;

  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;

}
.gn-dialog__title,

.gn-dialog__close,
.gn-sheet__close {
```

```ts
export { Avatar, initialsOf, type AvatarProps } from './components/avatar.js';
export { Button, type ButtonProps, type ButtonVariant } from './components/button.js';
export { Card, type CardProps } from './components/card.js';
export { Chip, type ChipProps, type ChipTone } from './components/chip.js';
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { DataSource, StreamItem } from '../src/data-source.js';
import { DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS } from '../src/view-types.js';
import { createFakeDataSource } from './helpers/fake-data-source.js';
import { submittedTicketCreated } from './helpers/seed.js';

describe('the fake DataSource (the harness every view test rides on)', () => {
  it('conforms to the DataSource interface', () => {
    const source: DataSource = createFakeDataSource();
    expect(typeof source.ingest).toBe('function');
    expect(typeof source.subscribe).toBe('function');
    expect(typeof source.query.getBoard).toBe('function');
  });

  it('records ingested batches and answers committed by default, index-aligned', async () => {
    const source = createFakeDataSource();
    const event: SubmittedEvent = submittedTicketCreated();
    const results = await source.ingest([event]);
    expect(source.ingested).toEqual([[event]]);
    expect(results).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'committed', eventId: event.id });
  });

  it('lets a test script refusals', async () => {
    const source = createFakeDataSource({}, (events) =>
      events.map((event) => ({
        status: 'refused' as const,
        eventId: event.id,
        rule: 'hierarchy.parent-terminal',
        reason: 'Parent is Done; it must reopen because it is acquiring live work.',
      })),
    );
    const [result] = await source.ingest([submittedTicketCreated()]);
    expect(result.status).toBe('refused');
  });

  it('fans stream items out to subscribers and honours unsubscribe', () => {
    const source = createFakeDataSource();
    const seen: StreamItem[] = [];
    const unsubscribe = source.subscribe('ws_1', 0, (item) => seen.push(item));
    expect(source.subscriptions).toEqual([{ workspaceId: 'ws_1', afterSeq: 0 }]);
    source.pushStreamItem({ type: 'caught-up', seq: 7 });
    unsubscribe();
    source.pushStreamItem({ type: 'caught-up', seq: 8 });
    expect(seen).toEqual([{ type: 'caught-up', seq: 7 }]);
  });

  it('serves overridable queries with honest empty defaults', async () => {
    const source = createFakeDataSource({ getMyWork: async () => ({ assigned: [], inTransit: [], contested: [] }) });
    expect(await source.query.getPreferences()).toEqual({ version: 1, projects: {}, savedFilters: [] });
    expect(DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS.grouping).toBe('epic');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/data-source.ts` — THE interface (exact contract file)**

```ts
import type { EventEnvelope, IngestResult, SubmittedEvent, WorkflowDef } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type {
  Actor,
  AttentionItem,
  BoardView,
  FilterSpec,
  Grouping,
  MyWorkView,
  SearchHit,
  TicketHistoryEvent,
  TicketListView,
  TicketRead,
  UserPreferences,
  WorkspaceRef,
} from './view-types.js';

/** One streamed frame, mirroring coordination amendment 13's server frames. */
export type StreamItem =
  | { type: 'event'; envelope: EventEnvelope }
  | { type: 'purge'; entityId: string }
  | { type: 'caught-up'; seq: number };

export type Unsubscribe = () => void;

/**
 * Typed read functions mirroring the server read surface (Plan 5 + earlier).
 * Implemented twice: online (this plan — HTTP) and replica (Plan 10 — local
 * store + outbox). Views call these and never speak HTTP (amendment 10).
 */
export type DataSourceQuery = {
  getMyWorkspaces(): Promise<WorkspaceRef[]>;
  getMembers(workspaceId: string): Promise<Actor[]>;
  getBoard(projectId: string): Promise<BoardView>;
  getTickets(projectId: string, filter: FilterSpec, grouping: Grouping): Promise<TicketListView>;
  getTicket(workspaceId: string, ticketId: string): Promise<TicketRead | null>;
  getTicketHistory(ticketId: string, afterSeq?: number, limit?: number): Promise<TicketHistoryEvent[]>;
  getWorkflowDef(workspaceId: string, workflowId: string, version?: number): Promise<WorkflowDef | null>;
  search(workspaceId: string, text: string, filter?: FilterSpec): Promise<SearchHit[]>;
  getMyWork(): Promise<MyWorkView>;
  getAttention(workspaceId: string): Promise<AttentionItem[]>;
  getPreferences(): Promise<UserPreferences>;
  putPreferences(preferences: UserPreferences): Promise<void>;
};

/** Coordination amendment 10 — the one seam between views and the world. */
export type DataSource = {
  ingest(events: SubmittedEvent[]): Promise<IngestResult[]>;
  query: DataSourceQuery;
  subscribe(workspaceId: string, afterSeq: number, cb: (item: StreamItem) => void): Unsubscribe;
};
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/view-types.ts`**

```ts
import type { FieldClaim, StateCategory, TicketState } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';

export type Actor = { id: string; name: string };
export type ProjectRef = { id: string; name: string };
export type WorkspaceRef = { id: string; name: string; projects: ProjectRef[] };

/**
 * Who claimed it and when — both clocks, always (spec §3: both are true in
 * different senses, and conflating them is a small lie). `actorName` is a
 * display convenience; a null name NEVER means anonymous — rendering falls
 * back to the raw actorId (see provenance.ts).
 */
export type ClaimProvenance = {
  actorId: string;
  actorName: string | null;
  clientTime: string;
  serverTime: string;
};

/** Owner decision: a reopen carries its cause; boards render it, never an anonymous flip. */
export type TransitionCause = { kind: 'reopened-for-child'; childTicketId: string };

/**
 * Derived child progress as shown to a viewer (prompt-pinned spelling).
 * Counts are over ALL direct children — visibility never shrinks them; the
 * children the viewer cannot see are NAMED in restrictedCount (spec §6).
 */
export type RollupToViewer = {
  totalChildren: number;
  terminalChildren: number;
  restrictedCount: number;
};

export type BoardCard = {
  ticketId: string;
  title: string;
  typeId: string;
  assignee: Actor | null;
  stateProvenance: ClaimProvenance;
  cause: TransitionCause | null;
  rollup: RollupToViewer | null;
  /** True only between submit and the server's answer (web is online-only). */
  pending: boolean;
};

/** Columns are exactly the attached workflow's states, never invented (spec §7). */
export type BoardColumn = {
  stateId: string;
  name: string;
  category: StateCategory;
  blocked: boolean;
  cards: BoardCard[];
};

/**
 * A project may hold ticket types on different workflows; merging their
 * states into one column set would invent columns no definition declares.
 * Boards therefore group by workflow.
 */
export type BoardGroup = {
  workflowId: string;
  workflowVersion: number;
  workflowName: string;
  columns: BoardColumn[];
};

export type BoardView = { projectId: string; groups: BoardGroup[] };

/** Composable filters (spec §7 Search): every axis optional, all AND-combined. */
export type FilterSpec = {
  text?: string;
  assigneeIds?: string[];
  collaboratorIds?: string[];
  typeIds?: string[];
  stateIds?: string[];
  stateCategories?: StateCategory[];
  projectIds?: string[];
  dueBefore?: string; // claimed dates only, YYYY-MM-DD — nothing estimated
  dueAfter?: string;
  fields?: Record<string, string>;
};

export type Grouping = 'epic' | 'status' | 'assignee' | 'type' | 'flat';

export type ListColumnId = 'status' | 'assignee' | 'dueDate' | 'type' | 'progress' | `field:${string}`;

export type ListRow = {
  ticketId: string;
  title: string;
  typeId: string;
  typeRank: number;
  parentId: string | null;
  /** Nearest rank-1 ancestor, for grouping by epic; null when none. */
  epicId: string | null;
  stateId: string;
  stateName: string;
  stateCategory: StateCategory;
  stateProvenance: ClaimProvenance;
  assignee: Actor | null;
  assignedProvenance: ClaimProvenance | null;
  dueDate: { date: string; claim: ClaimProvenance } | null;
  fields: Record<string, FieldClaim>;
  rollup: RollupToViewer | null;
  hasRestrictedParent: boolean;
};

export type TicketListView = { projectId: string; rows: ListRow[] };

/** Plan 4's read shape: a restricted parent is existence-without-title, structurally. */
export type ParentRef =
  | { kind: 'ticket'; id: string; title: string }
  | { kind: 'restricted'; id: string }
  | null;

export type TicketRead = {
  ticket: TicketState;
  title: string;
  stateProvenance: ClaimProvenance;
  parent: ParentRef;
  rollup: RollupToViewer | null;
};

/** Plan 3's history event: the ledger filtered, both clocks, no summarising. */
export type TicketHistoryEvent = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  version: number;
  payload: unknown;
  actorId: string;
  origin: string;
  clientTime: string;
  serverTime: string;
  seq: number;
};

export type WorkItem = {
  ticketId: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  projectId: string;
  projectName: string;
  title: string;
  stateId: string;
  stateName: string;
  stateCategory: StateCategory;
  stateProvenance: ClaimProvenance;
  dueDate: { date: string; claim: ClaimProvenance } | null;
};

export type ContestedClaim = {
  ticketId: string;
  title: string;
  rule: string;
  reason: string;
  claim: ClaimProvenance;
};

export type MyWorkView = {
  assigned: WorkItem[];
  inTransit: WorkItem[];
  contested: ContestedClaim[];
};

export type AttentionKind = 'blocked' | 'stale' | 'due-soon' | 'contested';

/** Computed, never authored (spec §7). `claim` is the underlying claim's provenance when one exists. */
export type AttentionItem = {
  kind: AttentionKind;
  ticketId: string;
  projectId: string;
  title: string;
  reason: string;
  claim: ClaimProvenance | null;
};

export type SearchHit = {
  ticketId: string;
  projectId: string;
  title: string;
  snippet: string;
  matchedIn: 'title' | 'description' | 'comment';
};

export type SavedFilter = { id: string; name: string; filter: FilterSpec };

export type ProjectViewPrefs = { columns: ListColumnId[]; grouping: Grouping };

/**
 * Server-owned per-user rows, NOT ledger events (amendment 14). View
 * preferences arrange truth; they never change it — proven in Task 10.
 */
export type UserPreferences = {
  version: 1;
  projects: Record<string, ProjectViewPrefs>;
  savedFilters: SavedFilter[];
};

export const DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS: ProjectViewPrefs = {
  columns: ['status', 'assignee', 'dueDate', 'progress'],
  grouping: 'epic',
};
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the fake DataSource and seed helpers**

`packages/app-core/test/helpers/fake-data-source.ts`:

```ts
import type { IngestResult, SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { DataSource, DataSourceQuery, StreamItem, Unsubscribe } from '../../src/data-source.js';

export type IngestScript = (events: SubmittedEvent[]) => IngestResult[] | Promise<IngestResult[]>;

export type FakeDataSource = DataSource & {
  ingested: SubmittedEvent[][];
  subscriptions: Array<{ workspaceId: string; afterSeq: number }>;
  pushStreamItem(item: StreamItem): void;
};

export function createFakeDataSource(
  overrides: Partial<DataSourceQuery> = {},
  ingestScript?: IngestScript,
): FakeDataSource {
  const listeners = new Set<(item: StreamItem) => void>();
  let nextSeq = 1;
  const query: DataSourceQuery = {
    getMyWorkspaces: async () => [],
    getMembers: async () => [],
    getBoard: async () => ({ projectId: 'prj_unset', groups: [] }),
    getTickets: async () => ({ projectId: 'prj_unset', rows: [] }),
    getTicket: async () => null,
    getTicketHistory: async () => [],
    getWorkflowDef: async () => null,
    search: async () => [],
    getMyWork: async () => ({ assigned: [], inTransit: [], contested: [] }),
    getAttention: async () => [],
    getPreferences: async () => ({ version: 1, projects: {}, savedFilters: [] }),
    putPreferences: async () => {},
    ...overrides,
  };
  const source: FakeDataSource = {
    ingested: [],
    subscriptions: [],
    async ingest(events) {
      source.ingested.push(events);
      if (ingestScript) return ingestScript(events);
      return events.map((event) => ({
        status: 'committed' as const,
        eventId: event.id,
        seq: nextSeq++,
        serverTime: '2026-08-20T09:00:00.000Z',
      }));
    },
    query,
    subscribe(workspaceId, afterSeq, cb): Unsubscribe {
      source.subscriptions.push({ workspaceId, afterSeq });
      listeners.add(cb);
      return () => listeners.delete(cb);
    },
    pushStreamItem(item) {
      for (const cb of [...listeners]) cb(item);
    },
  };
  return source;
}
```

Run: `grep -rn "IngestResult" packages/domain/src packages/domain/dist 2>/dev/null | head -5`

If `@gisik-nokma/domain` already exports `IngestResult`, skip to Step 2. Otherwise create `packages/domain/src/ingest-result.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The client-facing shape of one ingest answer. Per coordination, this type
 * lives in domain so clients import it without server code; apps/server's
 * ingest service produces it. The shape is the series contract, verbatim.
 */
export type IngestErrorCode =
  | 'malformed-envelope'
  | 'unknown-event-type'
  | 'unknown-event-version'
  | 'invalid-payload'
  | 'duplicate-event-id';

export type IngestResult =
  | { status: 'committed'; eventId: string; seq: number; serverTime: string }
  | { status: 'invalid'; eventId: string | null; code: IngestErrorCode; message: string }
  | { status: 'refused'; eventId: string; rule: string; reason: string };
```

Re-export from `packages/domain/src/index.ts`:


```ts
const BLOCK_TYPES = new Set(['paragraph', 'heading', 'blockquote', 'listItem', 'codeBlock']);

/**
 * Dependency-free plain-text walk of an editor document (ProseMirror JSON —
 * opaque outside packages/editor; coordination amendment 12). Used for
 * read-only rendering, search, and notifications. Never throws: unknown
 * shapes yield ''.
 */
export function docToPlainText(doc: unknown): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  walk(doc, parts);
  return parts.join('').replace(/\n{2,}/g, '\n').trim();
}

function walk(node: unknown, parts: string[]): void {
  if (node === null || typeof node !== 'object') return;
  if (Array.isArray(node)) {
    for (const child of node) walk(child, parts);
    return;
  }
  const record = node as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof record.text === 'string') parts.push(record.text);
  if (record.type === 'mention' && record.attrs !== null && typeof record.attrs === 'object') {
    const userId = (record.attrs as Record<string, unknown>).userId;
    if (typeof userId === 'string') parts.push(`@${userId}`);
  }
  if (record.type === 'hard_break') parts.push('\n');
  walk(record.content, parts);
  if (typeof record.type === 'string' && BLOCK_TYPES.has(record.type)) parts.push('\n');
}
```

Re-export from `packages/domain/src/index.ts`:

```ts
export { docToPlainText } from './doc-text.js';
```

`packages/app-core/src/submit.ts`:

```ts
import { newUlid, type SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';

export type EnvelopeSeed = Pick<SubmittedEvent, 'workspaceId' | 'entity' | 'type' | 'payload'> & {
  version?: number;
};

export type MakeSubmitted = (seed: EnvelopeSeed) => SubmittedEvent;

/**
 * Every claim the web client makes leaves through this factory: fresh ULID,
 * the signed-in actor, origin 'web', the device clock as the claim it is.
 * serverTime and seq are deliberately absent — the server assigns facts.
 */
export function makeSubmittedFactory(deps: {
  actorId: string;
  origin: SubmittedEvent['origin'];
  now?: () => Date;
}): MakeSubmitted {
  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date());
  return (seed) => ({
    id: newUlid(),
    workspaceId: seed.workspaceId,
    entity: seed.entity,
    type: seed.type,
    version: seed.version ?? 1,
    payload: seed.payload,
    actorId: deps.actorId,
    origin: deps.origin,
    clientTime: now().toISOString(),
  });
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/provenance.ts`:

```ts
import type { ClaimProvenance } from './view-types.js';

/**
 * Honesty contract 1: the UI never launders a claim into an anonymous fact.
 * A missing display name falls back to the raw actor id — attributed, never
 * blank, never a fabricated "system".
 */
export function actorLabel(actorId: string, actorName: string | null | undefined): string {
  const name = actorName?.trim();
  return name !== undefined && name.length > 0 ? name : actorId;
}

const MONTHS = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

/**
 * Deterministic UTC rendering. v1 shows the recorded instant in UTC rather
 * than guessing a viewer zone; the zone is NAMED wherever a time of day
 * appears, so nothing is silently shifted. (Viewer-zone display is later
 * polish; determinism and honesty come first.)
 */
export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return iso; // render the claim as claimed, never invent a date
  return `${date.getUTCDate()} ${MONTHS[date.getUTCMonth()]} ${date.getUTCFullYear()}`;
}

export function formatDateTime(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return iso;
  const hours = String(date.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0');
  const minutes = String(date.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${formatDate(iso)}, ${hours}:${minutes} UTC`;
}

/**
 * The unfold text (spec §7 Board): "marked Done by Anselma Marak on 19 Aug
 * 2026". Uses the server-recorded clock — the history view shows both clocks.
 */
export function formatStateProvenance(stateName: string, claim: ClaimProvenance): string {
  return `marked ${stateName} by ${actorLabel(claim.actorId, claim.actorName)} on ${formatDate(claim.serverTime)}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `cache.ts` and `cache-feed.ts`**

`packages/app-core/src/cache.ts`:

```ts
import type { EventEnvelope, TicketState, WorkflowDef } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';

/**
 * A best-effort snapshot of what this client has SEEN. It exists only to fuel
 * the local rule-engine pre-check (instant, named-reason refusal previews).
 * It is never rendered as truth and never substitutes for the server's
 * verdict: missing coverage downgrades the pre-check to "not evaluated" — it
 * never fakes state, and any stream event touching an entity drops the
 * snapshot rather than patching it optimistically.
 */
export type ClientStateCache = {
  upsertTicket(state: TicketState): void;
  upsertWorkflowDef(def: WorkflowDef): void;
  getTicket(ticketId: string): TicketState | undefined;
  getWorkflowDef(workflowId: string, version?: number): WorkflowDef | undefined;
  /** DIRECT children among cached tickets of one parent. */
  childrenOf(parentId: string): TicketState[];
  noteEvent(envelope: EventEnvelope): void;
};

export function createClientStateCache(): ClientStateCache {
  const tickets = new Map<string, TicketState>();
  const workflows = new Map<string, Map<number, WorkflowDef>>();
  return {
    upsertTicket(state) {
      tickets.set(state.id, state);
    },
    upsertWorkflowDef(def) {
      const versions = workflows.get(def.id) ?? new Map<number, WorkflowDef>();
      versions.set(def.version, def);
      workflows.set(def.id, versions);
    },
    getTicket: (ticketId) => tickets.get(ticketId),
    getWorkflowDef(workflowId, version) {
      const versions = workflows.get(workflowId);
      if (versions === undefined) return undefined;
      if (version !== undefined) return versions.get(version);
      let latest: WorkflowDef | undefined;
      for (const def of versions.values()) {
        if (latest === undefined || def.version > latest.version) latest = def;
      }
      return latest;
    },
    childrenOf(parentId) {
      return [...tickets.values()].filter((ticket) => ticket.parentId === parentId);
    },
    noteEvent(envelope) {
      if (envelope.entity.kind === 'ticket') tickets.delete(envelope.entity.id);
      if (envelope.entity.kind === 'workflow') workflows.delete(envelope.entity.id);
    },
  };
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/cache-feed.ts`:

```ts
import type { DataSource } from './data-source.js';
import type { ClientStateCache } from './cache.js';

/**
 * Decorator: an ordinary DataSource whose getTicket / getWorkflowDef results
 * feed the pre-check cache, and whose stream frames invalidate it. Views stay
 * ignorant of caching; the shell composes this once.
 */
export function withCacheFeed(source: DataSource, cache: ClientStateCache): DataSource {
  return {
    ingest: (events) => source.ingest(events),
    query: {
      ...source.query,
      async getTicket(workspaceId, ticketId) {
        const read = await source.query.getTicket(workspaceId, ticketId);
        if (read !== null) cache.upsertTicket(read.ticket);
        return read;
      },
      async getWorkflowDef(workspaceId, workflowId, version) {
        const def = await source.query.getWorkflowDef(workspaceId, workflowId, version);
        if (def !== null) cache.upsertWorkflowDef(def);
        return def;
      },
    },
    subscribe(workspaceId, afterSeq, cb) {
      return source.subscribe(workspaceId, afterSeq, (item) => {
        if (item.type === 'event') cache.noteEvent(item.envelope);
        cb(item);
      });
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createClientStateCache } from '../src/cache.js';
import { PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE, precheckEvent } from '../src/precheck.js';
import { makeSubmittedFactory } from '../src/submit.js';
import { PARENT, TICKET, WF_ERRAND, WS, ticketStateSeed } from './helpers/seed.js';

const make = makeSubmittedFactory({ actorId: 'user_kiro', origin: 'web' });

function transitionTo(toStateId: string, extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return make({
    workspaceId: WS,
    entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: TICKET },
    type: 'ticket.transitioned',
    payload: { toStateId, ...extras },
  });
}

describe('precheckEvent — instant named-reason previews from the same engine the server runs', () => {
  it('previews a refusal with the rule named: require-comment without a comment', async () => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    cache.upsertTicket(ticketStateSeed({ id: TICKET, stateId: 'doing', stateCategory: 'active' }));
    cache.upsertWorkflowDef(WF_ERRAND);
    const result = await precheckEvent(transitionTo('done'), cache);
    expect(result.kind).toBe('refused');
    if (result.kind === 'refused') {
      expect(result.rule).toBe('require-comment');
      expect(result.reason.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it('passes the same transition once the comment rides along', async () => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    cache.upsertTicket(ticketStateSeed({ id: TICKET, stateId: 'doing', stateCategory: 'active' }));
    cache.upsertWorkflowDef(WF_ERRAND);
    const result = await precheckEvent(transitionTo('done', { comment: 'filed at the portal' }), cache);
    expect(result.kind).toBe('passed');
  });

  it('downgrades to not-evaluated when the ticket is not cached — it never fakes state', async () => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    const result = await precheckEvent(transitionTo('done'), cache);
    expect(result).toEqual({ kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' });
  });

  it('downgrades a roles-based restrict-to refusal — memberships live server-side', async () => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    cache.upsertTicket(ticketStateSeed({ id: TICKET, stateId: 'doing', stateCategory: 'active' }));
    cache.upsertWorkflowDef({
      ...WF_ERRAND,
      transitions: WF_ERRAND.transitions.map((transition) =>
        transition.to === 'done'
          ? { ...transition, rules: [{ kind: 'restrict-to', roles: ['admin'] }] }
          : transition,
      ),
    });
    const fakeEvaluate = async () => ({ refused: true as const, rule: 'restrict-to', reason: 'not allowed' });
    const result = await precheckEvent(transitionTo('done'), cache, { evaluate: fakeEvaluate });
    expect(result).toEqual({ kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' });
  });

  it('previews hierarchy.parent-terminal on create under a cached terminal parent — the reopen moment', async () => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    cache.upsertTicket(ticketStateSeed({ id: PARENT, stateId: 'done', stateCategory: 'terminal' }));
    const created = make({
      workspaceId: WS,
      entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: '01J9GNEW000000000000000000' },
      type: 'ticket.created',
      payload: { typeId: 'task', projectId: 'prj', parentId: PARENT, title: 'Follow-up' },
    });
    const result = await precheckEvent(created, cache);
    expect(result.kind).toBe('refused');
    if (result.kind === 'refused') {
      expect(result.rule).toBe(PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE);
      expect(result.reason).toContain('reopen');
    }
  });

  it('passes create under a cached non-terminal parent', async () => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    cache.upsertTicket(ticketStateSeed({ id: PARENT, stateId: 'doing', stateCategory: 'active' }));
    const created = make({
      workspaceId: WS,
      entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: '01J9GNEW000000000000000001' },
      type: 'ticket.created',
      payload: { typeId: 'task', projectId: 'prj', parentId: PARENT, title: 'Follow-up' },
    });
    expect((await precheckEvent(created, cache)).kind).toBe('passed');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `precheck.ts`**

```ts
import { evaluateEvent, type EngineContext } from '@gisik-nokma/rule-engine';
import type { EventEnvelope, SubmittedEvent, TicketState } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { ClientStateCache } from './cache.js';

/** The owner decision's rule id for live children offered under terminal parents. */
export const PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE = 'hierarchy.parent-terminal';

export type PrecheckResult =
  | { kind: 'refused'; rule: string; reason: string }
  | { kind: 'passed' }
  | { kind: 'not-evaluated'; why: 'missing-local-state' };

export type PrecheckDeps = {
  evaluate?: typeof evaluateEvent;
  now?: () => Date;
};

/**
 * Instant, named-reason refusal preview (spec honesty contract 2), advisory
 * only: the server remains the authority, and when the two disagree the
 * server's refusal renders verbatim. Coverage the cache cannot honestly
 * provide downgrades to not-evaluated — the pre-check never fakes state.
 */
export async function precheckEvent(
  submitted: SubmittedEvent,
  cache: ClientStateCache,
  deps: PrecheckDeps = {},
): Promise<PrecheckResult> {
  if (submitted.entity.kind !== 'ticket') return { kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' };

  if (submitted.type === 'ticket.created' || submitted.type === 'ticket.reparented') {
    return precheckParentTerminal(submitted, cache);
  }

  if (submitted.type === 'ticket.transitioned') {
    const ticket = cache.getTicket(submitted.entity.id);
    const def = ticket === undefined ? undefined : cache.getWorkflowDef(ticket.workflowId);
    if (ticket === undefined || def === undefined) {
      return { kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' };
    }
    const evaluate = deps.evaluate ?? evaluateEvent;
    const verdict = await evaluate(completeForPrecheck(submitted, deps.now), contextFromCache(cache));
    if (!verdict.refused) return { kind: 'passed' };
    if (verdict.rule === 'restrict-to' && restrictToNeedsMembership(ticket, cache, submitted)) {
      // Role-based restrictions resolve against memberships only the server
      // holds; refusing here would be a guess wearing a uniform.
      return { kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' };
    }
    return { kind: 'refused', rule: verdict.rule, reason: verdict.reason };
  }

  return { kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' };
}

/** Provisional completion: serverTime/seq are the server's; these stand in only inside the local evaluation. */
export function completeForPrecheck(submitted: SubmittedEvent, now: () => Date = () => new Date()): EventEnvelope {
  return { ...submitted, serverTime: now().toISOString(), seq: 0 };
}

export function contextFromCache(cache: ClientStateCache): EngineContext {
  return {
    async getEntityState(kind, id) {
      if (kind !== 'ticket') return null;
      return cache.getTicket(id) ?? null;
    },
    async getChildrenStates(ticketId) {
      return cache.childrenOf(ticketId);
    },
    async getWorkflowDef(workflowId, version) {
      return cache.getWorkflowDef(workflowId, version) ?? null;
    },
  };
}

function precheckParentTerminal(submitted: SubmittedEvent, cache: ClientStateCache): PrecheckResult {
  const payload = submitted.payload as { parentId?: string | null; newParentId?: string | null } | null;
  const parentId = submitted.type === 'ticket.created' ? payload?.parentId : payload?.newParentId;
  if (parentId === null || parentId === undefined) return { kind: 'passed' };
  const parent = cache.getTicket(parentId);
  if (parent === undefined) return { kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' };
  if (parent.stateCategory !== 'terminal') return { kind: 'passed' };
  return {
    kind: 'refused',
    rule: PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE,
    reason: `${titleOf(parent)} is in a terminal state; it must reopen because it is acquiring live work.`,
  };
}

function restrictToNeedsMembership(
  ticket: TicketState,
  cache: ClientStateCache,
  submitted: SubmittedEvent,
): boolean {
  const def = cache.getWorkflowDef(ticket.workflowId);
  const toStateId = (submitted.payload as { toStateId?: string } | null)?.toStateId;
  if (def === undefined || toStateId === undefined) return true;
  return def.transitions
    .filter((transition) => (transition.from === ticket.stateId || transition.from === '*') && transition.to === toStateId)
    .flatMap((transition) => transition.rules)
    .some((rule) => rule.kind === 'restrict-to' && Array.isArray((rule as { roles?: string[] }).roles) && ((rule as { roles?: string[] }).roles?.length ?? 0) > 0);
}

function titleOf(ticket: TicketState): string {
  const value = ticket.fields.title?.value;
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 ? `"${value}"` : `Ticket ${ticket.id}`;
}
```

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

export type HttpHit = { file: string; line: number; match: string };

/** packages/app-core/test/helpers → three levels up is the package root. */
export const APP_CORE_SRC = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../src', import.meta.url));

const FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS: ReadonlyArray<{ label: string; pattern: RegExp }> = [
  { label: 'online import', pattern: /from\s+['"][^'"]*\/online(?:\/|['"])/ },
  { label: 'fetch call', pattern: /\bfetch\s*\(/ },
  { label: 'WebSocket construction', pattern: /\bnew\s+WebSocket\b/ },
  { label: 'XMLHttpRequest', pattern: /\bXMLHttpRequest\b/ },
  { label: 'EventSource construction', pattern: /\bnew\s+EventSource\b/ },
];

export function findHttpUsage(content: string): string[] {
  return FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS.filter(({ pattern }) => pattern.test(content)).map(({ label }) => label);
}

/** Scans src/ excluding src/online — the one module allowed to speak HTTP. */
export function scanForHttpOutsideOnline(): HttpHit[] {
  const hits: HttpHit[] = [];
  walk(APP_CORE_SRC, hits);
  return hits;
}

function walk(directory: string, hits: HttpHit[]): void {
  for (const entry of readdirSync(directory)) {
    const path = join(directory, entry);
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) {
      if (entry !== 'online') walk(path, hits);
      continue;
    }
    if (!entry.endsWith('.ts') && !entry.endsWith('.tsx')) continue;
    const lines = readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split('\n');
    lines.forEach((line, index) => {
      for (const match of findHttpUsage(line)) {
        hits.push({ file: relative(APP_CORE_SRC, path).split(sep).join('/'), line: index + 1, match });
      }
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/online/http.ts`**

```ts
export type FetchLike = (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

export type HttpClient = {
  get(path: string): Promise<unknown>;
  post(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
  put(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
};

export class HttpError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  constructor(status: number, path: string) {
    super(`HTTP ${status} for ${path}`);
    this.name = 'HttpError';
    this.status = status;
  }
}

/**
 * Thin JSON transport. `credentials: 'include'` carries the better-auth
 * session cookie. 404 answers null (absence is an answer, not an exception —
 * and for ticket reads, indistinguishable-from-nonexistent is Plan 4's
 * deliberate design); other non-2xx statuses throw HttpError.
 */
export function createHttpClient(baseUrl: string, fetchImpl: FetchLike): HttpClient {
  async function send(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    const response = await fetchImpl(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
      method,
      credentials: 'include',
      headers: body === undefined ? undefined : { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    if (response.status === 404) return null;
    if (!response.ok) throw new HttpError(response.status, path);
    if (response.status === 204) return undefined;
    return response.json();
  }
  return {
    get: (path) => send('GET', path),
    post: (path, body) => send('POST', path, body),
    put: (path, body) => send('PUT', path, body),
  };
}
```
