- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `src/online/mappers.ts`**

```ts
import type { WorkflowDef } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { IngestResult } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type {
  Actor,
  AttentionItem,
  BoardCard,
  BoardColumn,
  BoardGroup,
  BoardView,
  ClaimProvenance,
  ListRow,
  MyWorkView,
  ParentRef,
  RollupToViewer,
  SearchHit,
  TicketHistoryEvent,
  TicketListView,
  TicketRead,
  UserPreferences,
  WorkspaceRef,
} from '../view-types.js';

/**
 * The ONE adaptation layer between Plan 5's wire JSON and the client contract
 * (see "Adaptation rule" in the plan header). Malformed responses throw
 * MappingError naming the path — a garbled read must fail loudly, never
 * render half-truths.
 */
export class MappingError extends Error {
  constructor(path: string, expected: string) {
    super(`response mapping failed at ${path}: expected ${expected}`);
    this.name = 'MappingError';
  }
}

function asRecord(value: unknown, path: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
    throw new MappingError(path, 'an object');
  }
  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function asArray(value: unknown, path: string): unknown[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new MappingError(path, 'an array');
  return value;
}

function asString(value: unknown, path: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new MappingError(path, 'a string');
  return value;
}

function asNumber(value: unknown, path: string): number {
  if (typeof value !== 'number') throw new MappingError(path, 'a number');
  return value;
}

function optionalString(value: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : null;
}

export function mapProvenance(raw: unknown, path = 'provenance'): ClaimProvenance {
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return {
    actorId: asString(record.actorId, `${path}.actorId`),
    actorName: optionalString(record.actorName),
    clientTime: asString(record.clientTime, `${path}.clientTime`),
    serverTime: asString(record.serverTime, `${path}.serverTime`),
  };
}

export function mapRollup(raw: unknown): RollupToViewer | null {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'rollup');
  const total = record.totalChildren ?? record.total;
  const terminal = record.terminalChildren ?? record.terminal;
  return {
    totalChildren: asNumber(total, 'rollup.totalChildren'),
    terminalChildren: asNumber(terminal, 'rollup.terminalChildren'),
    restrictedCount: typeof record.restrictedCount === 'number' ? record.restrictedCount : 0,
  };
}

function mapActor(raw: unknown, path: string): Actor | null {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return { id: asString(record.id, `${path}.id`), name: asString(record.name, `${path}.name`) };
}

export function mapActors(raw: unknown): Actor[] {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'members');
  return asArray(record.members ?? raw, 'members').map((entry, index) => {
    const actor = mapActor(entry, `members[${index}]`);
    if (actor === null) throw new MappingError(`members[${index}]`, 'an actor');
    return actor;
  });
}

export function mapWorkspaceRefs(raw: unknown): WorkspaceRef[] {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'workspaces');
  return asArray(record.workspaces ?? raw, 'workspaces').map((entry, index) => {
    const workspace = asRecord(entry, `workspaces[${index}]`);
    return {
      id: asString(workspace.id, `workspaces[${index}].id`),
      name: asString(workspace.name, `workspaces[${index}].name`),
      projects: asArray(workspace.projects ?? [], `workspaces[${index}].projects`).map((project, projectIndex) => {
        const projectRecord = asRecord(project, `workspaces[${index}].projects[${projectIndex}]`);
        return {
          id: asString(projectRecord.id, 'project.id'),
          name: asString(projectRecord.name, 'project.name'),
        };
      }),
    };
  });
}

function mapCause(raw: unknown): BoardCard['cause'] {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'cause');
  if (record.kind !== 'reopened-for-child') return null;
  return { kind: 'reopened-for-child', childTicketId: asString(record.childTicketId, 'cause.childTicketId') };
}

function mapBoardCard(raw: unknown, path: string): BoardCard {
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return {
    ticketId: asString(record.ticketId, `${path}.ticketId`),
    title: asString(record.title, `${path}.title`),
    typeId: asString(record.typeId, `${path}.typeId`),
    assignee: mapActor(record.assignee, `${path}.assignee`),
    stateProvenance: mapProvenance(record.stateProvenance, `${path}.stateProvenance`),
    cause: mapCause(record.cause),
    rollup: mapRollup(record.rollup),
    pending: false, // pending is client-side truth: set only between submit and the server's answer
  };
}

function mapBoardColumn(raw: unknown, path: string): BoardColumn {
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return {
    stateId: asString(record.stateId, `${path}.stateId`),
    name: asString(record.name, `${path}.name`),
    category: asString(record.category, `${path}.category`) as BoardColumn['category'],
    blocked: record.blocked === true,
    cards: asArray(record.cards ?? [], `${path}.cards`).map((card, index) => mapBoardCard(card, `${path}.cards[${index}]`)),
  };
}

function mapBoardGroup(raw: unknown, path: string): BoardGroup {
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return {
    workflowId: asString(record.workflowId, `${path}.workflowId`),
    workflowVersion: asNumber(record.workflowVersion, `${path}.workflowVersion`),
    workflowName: asString(record.workflowName, `${path}.workflowName`),
    columns: asArray(record.columns ?? [], `${path}.columns`).map((column, index) =>
      mapBoardColumn(column, `${path}.columns[${index}]`),
    ),
  };
}

export function mapBoardView(raw: unknown): BoardView {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'board');
  return {
    projectId: asString(record.projectId ?? 'unknown', 'board.projectId'),
    groups: asArray(record.groups, 'board.groups').map((group, index) => mapBoardGroup(group, `board.groups[${index}]`)),
  };
}

function mapDueDate(raw: unknown, path: string): ListRow['dueDate'] {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return {
    date: asString(record.date, `${path}.date`),
    claim: mapProvenance(record.claim, `${path}.claim`),
  };
}

function mapListRow(raw: unknown, path: string): ListRow {
  const record = asRecord(raw, path);
  return {
    ticketId: asString(record.ticketId, `${path}.ticketId`),
    title: asString(record.title, `${path}.title`),
    typeId: asString(record.typeId, `${path}.typeId`),
    typeRank: asNumber(record.typeRank ?? 0, `${path}.typeRank`),
    parentId: optionalString(record.parentId),
    epicId: optionalString(record.epicId),
    stateId: asString(record.stateId, `${path}.stateId`),
    stateName: asString(record.stateName, `${path}.stateName`),
    stateCategory: asString(record.stateCategory, `${path}.stateCategory`) as ListRow['stateCategory'],
    stateProvenance: mapProvenance(record.stateProvenance, `${path}.stateProvenance`),
    assignee: mapActor(record.assignee, `${path}.assignee`),
    assignedProvenance:
      record.assignedProvenance === null || record.assignedProvenance === undefined
        ? null
        : mapProvenance(record.assignedProvenance, `${path}.assignedProvenance`),
    dueDate: mapDueDate(record.dueDate, `${path}.dueDate`),
    fields: (record.fields ?? {}) as ListRow['fields'],
    rollup: mapRollup(record.rollup),
    hasRestrictedParent: record.hasRestrictedParent === true,
  };
}

export function mapListView(raw: unknown): TicketListView {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'tickets');
  return {
    projectId: asString(record.projectId ?? 'unknown', 'tickets.projectId'),
    rows: asArray(record.rows ?? record.tickets, 'tickets.rows').map((row, index) => mapListRow(row, `tickets.rows[${index}]`)),
  };
}

function mapParent(raw: unknown): ParentRef {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'parent');
  if (record.kind === 'restricted') return { kind: 'restricted', id: asString(record.id, 'parent.id') };
  return {
    kind: 'ticket',
    id: asString(record.id, 'parent.id'),
    title: asString(record.title, 'parent.title'),
  };
}

export function mapTicketRead(raw: unknown): TicketRead {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'ticketRead');
  const ticket = asRecord(record.ticket, 'ticketRead.ticket') as unknown as TicketRead['ticket'];
  const title = typeof (ticket.fields?.title?.value) === 'string' ? (ticket.fields.title.value as string) : ticket.id;
  const stateProvenance =
    record.stateProvenance === undefined
      ? provenanceFromFields(ticket)
      : mapProvenance(record.stateProvenance, 'ticketRead.stateProvenance');
  return {
    ticket,
    title,
    stateProvenance,
    parent: mapParent(record.parent),
    rollup: mapRollup(record.rollup),
  };
}

/** Fallback when the read model omits a dedicated state-provenance block: the
 * last transition's actor from the state's own log, clocks from the title
 * claim if that is all we hold. History remains the full record. */
function provenanceFromFields(ticket: TicketRead['ticket']): ClaimProvenance {
  const lastTransition = ticket.transitions[ticket.transitions.length - 1];
  const titleClaim = ticket.fields.title;
  return {
    actorId: lastTransition?.actorId ?? titleClaim?.actorId ?? 'unknown-actor',
    actorName: null,
    clientTime: titleClaim?.clientTime ?? '',
    serverTime: titleClaim?.serverTime ?? '',
  };
}

export function mapHistory(raw: unknown): TicketHistoryEvent[] {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'history');
  return asArray(record.events, 'history.events').map((entry, index) => {
    const event = asRecord(entry, `history.events[${index}]`);
    return {
      id: asString(event.id, 'event.id'),
      type: asString(event.type, 'event.type'),
      version: asNumber(event.version, 'event.version'),
      payload: event.payload,
      actorId: asString(event.actorId, 'event.actorId'),
      origin: asString(event.origin, 'event.origin'),
      clientTime: asString(event.clientTime, 'event.clientTime'),
      serverTime: asString(event.serverTime, 'event.serverTime'),
      seq: asNumber(event.seq, 'event.seq'),
    };
  });
}

export function mapWorkflowDef(raw: unknown): WorkflowDef {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'workflow');
  return (record.definition ?? record.workflow ?? raw) as WorkflowDef;
}

export function mapSearchHits(raw: unknown): SearchHit[] {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'search');
  return asArray(record.hits ?? record.results, 'search.hits').map((entry, index) => {
    const hit = asRecord(entry, `search.hits[${index}]`);
    return {
      ticketId: asString(hit.ticketId, 'hit.ticketId'),
      projectId: asString(hit.projectId, 'hit.projectId'),
      title: asString(hit.title, 'hit.title'),
      snippet: asString(hit.snippet ?? '', 'hit.snippet'),
      matchedIn: (hit.matchedIn ?? 'title') as SearchHit['matchedIn'],
    };
  });
}

export function mapMyWork(raw: unknown): MyWorkView {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'myWork');
  const workItem = (entry: unknown, path: string) => {
    const item = asRecord(entry, path);
    return {
      ticketId: asString(item.ticketId, `${path}.ticketId`),
      workspaceId: asString(item.workspaceId, `${path}.workspaceId`),
      projectId: asString(item.projectId, `${path}.projectId`),
      projectName: asString(item.projectName ?? '', `${path}.projectName`),
      title: asString(item.title, `${path}.title`),
      stateId: asString(item.stateId, `${path}.stateId`),
      stateName: asString(item.stateName, `${path}.stateName`),
      stateCategory: asString(item.stateCategory, `${path}.stateCategory`) as MyWorkView['assigned'][number]['stateCategory'],
      stateProvenance: mapProvenance(item.stateProvenance, `${path}.stateProvenance`),
      dueDate: mapDueDate(item.dueDate, `${path}.dueDate`),
    };
  };
  return {
    assigned: asArray(record.assigned ?? [], 'myWork.assigned').map((entry, index) => workItem(entry, `myWork.assigned[${index}]`)),
    inTransit: asArray(record.inTransit ?? [], 'myWork.inTransit').map((entry, index) => workItem(entry, `myWork.inTransit[${index}]`)),
    contested: asArray(record.contested ?? [], 'myWork.contested').map((entry, index) => {
      const item = asRecord(entry, `myWork.contested[${index}]`);
      return {
        ticketId: asString(item.ticketId, 'contested.ticketId'),
        title: asString(item.title, 'contested.title'),
        rule: asString(item.rule, 'contested.rule'),
        reason: asString(item.reason, 'contested.reason'),
        claim: mapProvenance(item.claim, 'contested.claim'),
      };
    }),
  };
}

export function mapAttention(raw: unknown): AttentionItem[] {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'attention');
  return asArray(record.items ?? raw, 'attention.items').map((entry, index) => {
    const item = asRecord(entry, `attention.items[${index}]`);
    return {
      kind: asString(item.kind, 'item.kind') as AttentionItem['kind'],
      ticketId: asString(item.ticketId, 'item.ticketId'),
      projectId: asString(item.projectId, 'item.projectId'),
      title: asString(item.title, 'item.title'),
      reason: asString(item.reason, 'item.reason'),
      claim: item.claim === null || item.claim === undefined ? null : mapProvenance(item.claim, 'item.claim'),
    };
  });
}

const DEFAULT_PREFERENCES: UserPreferences = { version: 1, projects: {}, savedFilters: [] };

export function mapPreferences(raw: unknown): UserPreferences {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined || typeof raw !== 'object') return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  const record = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (record.version !== 1) return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  return record as unknown as UserPreferences;
}

export function mapIngestResults(raw: unknown, submittedCount: number): IngestResult[] {
  const record = asRecord(raw, 'ingest');
  const results = asArray(record.results, 'ingest.results');
  if (results.length !== submittedCount) {
    throw new MappingError('ingest.results', `${submittedCount} index-aligned results (got ${results.length})`);
  }
  return results as IngestResult[];
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createOnlineDataSource, listQueryString } from '../../src/online/online-data-source.js';
import { submittedTicketCreated } from '../helpers/seed.js';

function recordingFetch(answers: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const calls: Array<{ input: string; init?: RequestInit }> = [];
  const fetchImpl = async (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({ input, init });
    const path = input.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/, '').split('?')[0];
    const body = answers[path];
    return {
      ok: body !== undefined,
      status: body === undefined ? 404 : 200,
      json: async () => body,
    } as Response;
  };
  return { fetchImpl, calls };
}

describe('createOnlineDataSource — paths verbatim from the pinned surface', () => {
  it('ingests through POST /v1/events and returns index-aligned results', async () => {
    const event = submittedTicketCreated();
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = recordingFetch({
      '/v1/events': { results: [{ status: 'committed', eventId: event.id, seq: 1, serverTime: 's' }] },
    });
    const source = createOnlineDataSource({ baseUrl: 'http://server.test', fetchImpl });
    const results = await source.ingest([event]);
    expect(results[0].status).toBe('committed');
    expect(calls[0].input).toBe('http://server.test/v1/events');
    expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init?.body)).events).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('refuses a batch over 100 client-side — the contract is 1..100', async () => {
    const { fetchImpl } = recordingFetch({});
    const source = createOnlineDataSource({ baseUrl: '', fetchImpl });
    const oversized = Array.from({ length: 101 }, () => submittedTicketCreated());
    await expect(source.ingest(oversized)).rejects.toThrow(/100/);
  });

  it("reads the single ticket through Plan 4's /api/ path, verbatim", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = recordingFetch({});
    const source = createOnlineDataSource({ baseUrl: 'http://server.test', fetchImpl });
    expect(await source.query.getTicket('ws_1', 't_1')).toBeNull();
    expect(calls[0].input).toBe('http://server.test/api/workspaces/ws_1/tickets/t_1');
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `src/online/online-data-source.ts` and `src/online/index.ts`**

```ts
// packages/app-core/src/online/online-data-source.ts
import type { DataSource, DataSourceQuery } from '../data-source.js';
import type { FilterSpec, Grouping } from '../view-types.js';
import { createHttpClient, type FetchLike } from './http.js';
import {
  mapActors,
  mapAttention,
  mapBoardView,
  mapHistory,
  mapIngestResults,
  mapListView,
  mapMyWork,
  mapPreferences,
  mapSearchHits,
  mapTicketRead,
  mapWorkflowDef,
  mapWorkspaceRefs,
} from './mappers.js';
import { createStreamSubscriber, type WebSocketLike } from './stream.js';

export const MAX_INGEST_BATCH = 100;

export type OnlineDataSourceDeps = {
  /** HTTP origin — '' when the shell proxies same-origin (Next rewrites). */
  baseUrl: string;
  /** WS origin; defaults to baseUrl. Next dev cannot proxy WS, so the web shell passes the server origin here. */
  streamBaseUrl?: string;
  fetchImpl?: FetchLike;
  webSocketFactory?: (url: string) => WebSocketLike;
  random?: () => number;
};

export function listQueryString(filter: FilterSpec, grouping: Grouping): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  params.set('grouping', grouping);
  if (filter.text !== undefined) params.set('q', filter.text);
  for (const id of filter.assigneeIds ?? []) params.append('assignee', id);
  for (const id of filter.collaboratorIds ?? []) params.append('collaborator', id);
  for (const id of filter.typeIds ?? []) params.append('type', id);
  for (const id of filter.stateIds ?? []) params.append('state', id);
  for (const category of filter.stateCategories ?? []) params.append('category', category);
  for (const id of filter.projectIds ?? []) params.append('project', id);
  if (filter.dueBefore !== undefined) params.set('dueBefore', filter.dueBefore);
  if (filter.dueAfter !== undefined) params.set('dueAfter', filter.dueAfter);
  for (const [fieldId, value] of Object.entries(filter.fields ?? {})) params.set(`field.${fieldId}`, value);
  return params.toString();
}

/** The online implementation of amendment 10's DataSource: HTTP + WS. */
export function createOnlineDataSource(deps: OnlineDataSourceDeps): DataSource {
  const fetchImpl: FetchLike = deps.fetchImpl ?? ((input, init) => fetch(input, init));
  const http = createHttpClient(deps.baseUrl, fetchImpl);
  const query: DataSourceQuery = {
    getMyWorkspaces: async () => mapWorkspaceRefs(await http.get('/v1/me/workspaces')),
    getMembers: async (workspaceId) => mapActors(await http.get(`/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/members`)),
    getBoard: async (projectId) => mapBoardView(await http.get(`/v1/projects/${projectId}/board`)),
    getTickets: async (projectId, filter, grouping) =>
      mapListView(await http.get(`/v1/projects/${projectId}/tickets?${listQueryString(filter, grouping)}`)),
    getTicket: async (workspaceId, ticketId) => {
      // Plan 4's path, verbatim — /api/, not /v1/ (see Consumed surface).
      const raw = await http.get(`/api/workspaces/${workspaceId}/tickets/${ticketId}`);
      return raw === null ? null : mapTicketRead(raw);
    },
    getTicketHistory: async (ticketId, afterSeq, limit) => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (afterSeq !== undefined) params.set('afterSeq', String(afterSeq));
      if (limit !== undefined) params.set('limit', String(limit));
      const suffix = params.size > 0 ? `?${params.toString()}` : '';
      const raw = await http.get(`/v1/tickets/${ticketId}/history${suffix}`);
      return raw === null ? [] : mapHistory(raw);
    },
    getWorkflowDef: async (workspaceId, workflowId, version) => {
      const suffix = version === undefined ? '' : `?version=${version}`;
      const raw = await http.get(`/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/workflows/${workflowId}${suffix}`);
      return raw === null ? null : mapWorkflowDef(raw);
    },
    search: async (workspaceId, text, filter) => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ workspaceId, q: text });
      const filterQuery = filter === undefined ? '' : `&${listQueryString(filter, 'flat')}`;
      const raw = await http.get(`/v1/search?${params.toString()}${filterQuery}`);
      return raw === null ? [] : mapSearchHits(raw);
    },
    getMyWork: async () => mapMyWork(await http.get('/v1/me/work')),
    getAttention: async (workspaceId) => {
      const raw = await http.get(`/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/attention`);
      return raw === null ? [] : mapAttention(raw);
    },
    getPreferences: async () => mapPreferences(await http.get('/v1/me/preferences')),
    putPreferences: async (preferences) => {
      await http.put('/v1/me/preferences', preferences);
    },
  };
  return {
    async ingest(events) {
      if (events.length === 0) return [];
      if (events.length > MAX_INGEST_BATCH) {
        throw new Error(`ingest batch of ${events.length} exceeds the contract maximum of ${MAX_INGEST_BATCH}`);
      }
      return mapIngestResults(await http.post('/v1/events', { events }), events.length);
    },
    query,
    subscribe: createStreamSubscriber({
      baseUrl: deps.streamBaseUrl ?? deps.baseUrl,
      webSocketFactory: deps.webSocketFactory,
      random: deps.random,
    }),
  };
}
```

### Task 8: Online stream — WS subscribe, reconnect with backoff, `afterSeq` resume

**Files:**

- Test: `packages/app-core/test/online/stream.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StreamItem`, `Unsubscribe` (Task 4); amendment 13's frames (header).
- Produces: `createStreamSubscriber(deps: StreamDeps)` → `(workspaceId, afterSeq, cb) => Unsubscribe`; `WebSocketLike` (injectable socket seam — Plan 10's Tauri/RN shells reuse it); `streamUrl(baseUrl)`; `backoffDelayMs(attempt, random)`; `BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS = 500`, `BACKOFF_CAP_MS = 30000`; `parseFrame(data)`.

`packages/app-core/test/online/stream.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { StreamItem } from '../../src/data-source.js';
import {
  BACKOFF_CAP_MS,
  BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS,
  backoffDelayMs,
  createStreamSubscriber,
  parseFrame,
  streamUrl,
  type WebSocketLike,
} from '../../src/online/stream.js';

class FakeWebSocket implements WebSocketLike {
  sent: string[] = [];
  closedByClient = false;
  onopen: ((event: unknown) => void) | null = null;
  onmessage: ((event: { data: unknown }) => void) | null = null;
  onclose: ((event: unknown) => void) | null = null;
  onerror: ((event: unknown) => void) | null = null;
  send(data: string): void {
    this.sent.push(data);
  }
  close(): void {
    this.closedByClient = true;
  }
  serverOpen(): void {
    this.onopen?.({});
  }
  serverSend(frame: unknown): void {
    this.onmessage?.({ data: JSON.stringify(frame) });
  }
  serverClose(): void {
    this.onclose?.({});
  }
}

function harness() {
  const sockets: FakeWebSocket[] = [];
  const subscribe = createStreamSubscriber({
    baseUrl: 'http://server.test',
    webSocketFactory: () => {
      const socket = new FakeWebSocket();
      sockets.push(socket);
      return socket;
    },
    random: () => 0.5, // jitter factor exactly 1.0
  });
  return { sockets, subscribe };
}

const ENVELOPE = {
  id: '01J9GEVT000000000000000000',
  workspaceId: 'ws_1',
  entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: 't_1' },
  type: 'ticket.assigned',
  version: 1,
  payload: { assigneeId: null },
  actorId: 'user_a',
  origin: 'web',
  clientTime: 'c',
  serverTime: 's',
  seq: 12,
};

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe('streamUrl and backoff', () => {
  it('translates the HTTP origin to ws and appends /v1/stream', () => {
    expect(streamUrl('http://server.test')).toBe('ws://server.test/v1/stream');
    expect(streamUrl('https://gisik.example')).toBe('wss://gisik.example/v1/stream');
  });

  it('doubles from 500ms and caps at 30s, jitter within ±20%', () => {
    const flat = (attempt: number) => backoffDelayMs(attempt, () => 0.5);
    expect(flat(0)).toBe(BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS);
    expect(flat(1)).toBe(1000);
    expect(flat(2)).toBe(2000);
    expect(flat(10)).toBe(BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
    expect(backoffDelayMs(0, () => 0)).toBe(400);
    expect(backoffDelayMs(0, () => 1)).toBe(600);
  });
});

describe('subscribe / resume protocol (amendment 13, verbatim frames)', () => {
  it('sends the subscribe frame on open and delivers event, purge and caught-up frames', () => {
    const { sockets, subscribe } = harness();
    const seen: StreamItem[] = [];
    subscribe('ws_1', 41, (item) => seen.push(item));
    sockets[0].serverOpen();
    expect(JSON.parse(sockets[0].sent[0])).toEqual({ type: 'subscribe', workspaceId: 'ws_1', afterSeq: 41 });
    sockets[0].serverSend({ type: 'event', envelope: ENVELOPE });
    sockets[0].serverSend({ type: 'purge', entityId: 't_gone' });
    sockets[0].serverSend({ type: 'caught-up', seq: 12 });
    expect(seen.map((item) => item.type)).toEqual(['event', 'purge', 'caught-up']);
  });

  it('answers ping with pong without delivering it', () => {
    const { sockets, subscribe } = harness();
    const seen: StreamItem[] = [];
    subscribe('ws_1', 0, (item) => seen.push(item));
    sockets[0].serverOpen();
    sockets[0].serverSend({ type: 'ping' });
    expect(sockets[0].sent).toContain('{"type":"pong"}');
    expect(seen).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reconnects after backoff and resumes from the last seen seq — not the original afterSeq', () => {
    const { sockets, subscribe } = harness();
    subscribe('ws_1', 0, () => {});
    sockets[0].serverOpen();
    sockets[0].serverSend({ type: 'event', envelope: ENVELOPE }); // seq 12
    sockets[0].serverClose();
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(1);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS);
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(2);
    sockets[1].serverOpen();
    expect(JSON.parse(sockets[1].sent[0])).toEqual({ type: 'subscribe', workspaceId: 'ws_1', afterSeq: 12 });
  });

  it('backs off exponentially across consecutive failures and resets after a successful open', () => {
    const { sockets, subscribe } = harness();
    subscribe('ws_1', 0, () => {});
    sockets[0].serverClose();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(500);
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(2);
    sockets[1].serverClose();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(999);
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(2); // second retry waits 1000ms
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(3);
    sockets[2].serverOpen(); // success resets the attempt counter
    sockets[2].serverClose();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(500);
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it('unsubscribe closes the socket and stops reconnecting', () => {
    const { sockets, subscribe } = harness();
    const unsubscribe = subscribe('ws_1', 0, () => {});
    sockets[0].serverOpen();
    unsubscribe();
    expect(sockets[0].closedByClient).toBe(true);
    sockets[0].serverClose();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000);
    expect(sockets).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('ignores unparseable and unknown frames — never guesses', () => {
    expect(parseFrame('not json')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseFrame(JSON.stringify({ type: 'mystery' }))).toBeNull();
    expect(parseFrame(42)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/online/stream.ts`**

```ts
import type { EventEnvelope } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { StreamItem, Unsubscribe } from '../data-source.js';

/** The subset of the DOM WebSocket the stream needs — injectable for tests and non-browser shells. */
export type WebSocketLike = {
  send(data: string): void;
  close(): void;
  onopen: ((event: unknown) => void) | null;
  onmessage: ((event: { data: unknown }) => void) | null;
  onclose: ((event: unknown) => void) | null;
  onerror: ((event: unknown) => void) | null;
};

export type StreamDeps = {
  baseUrl: string;
  webSocketFactory?: (url: string) => WebSocketLike;
  random?: () => number;
};

export const BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS = 500;
export const BACKOFF_CAP_MS = 30_000;

export function streamUrl(baseUrl: string): string {
  return `${baseUrl.replace(/^http/, 'ws')}/v1/stream`;
}

/** Exponential with ±20% jitter: honest retry pressure without thundering herds. */
export function backoffDelayMs(attempt: number, random: () => number): number {
  const base = Math.min(BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS * 2 ** attempt, BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
  const jitterFactor = 0.8 + random() * 0.4;
  return Math.round(base * jitterFactor);
}

type PingFrame = { type: 'ping' };

export function parseFrame(data: unknown): StreamItem | PingFrame | null {
  if (typeof data !== 'string') return null;
  let raw: unknown;
  try {
    raw = JSON.parse(data);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (raw === null || typeof raw !== 'object') return null;
  const frame = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (frame.type === 'ping') return { type: 'ping' };
  if (frame.type === 'event' && frame.envelope !== null && typeof frame.envelope === 'object') {
    return { type: 'event', envelope: frame.envelope as EventEnvelope };
  }
  if (frame.type === 'purge' && typeof frame.entityId === 'string') {
    return { type: 'purge', entityId: frame.entityId };
  }
  if (frame.type === 'caught-up' && typeof frame.seq === 'number') {
    return { type: 'caught-up', seq: frame.seq };
  }
  return null; // unknown frames are ignored, never guessed at
}

/**
 * Amendment 13's client half. Reconnects forever with capped jittered
 * backoff; every (re)subscribe resumes from the highest seq actually seen, so
 * a flaky link never re-plays what the client already holds — and never skips
 * what it does not.
 */
export function createStreamSubscriber(deps: StreamDeps) {
  const factory = deps.webSocketFactory ?? ((url: string) => new WebSocket(url) as unknown as WebSocketLike);
  const random = deps.random ?? Math.random;

  return function subscribe(
    workspaceId: string,
    afterSeq: number,
    cb: (item: StreamItem) => void,
  ): Unsubscribe {
    let lastSeq = afterSeq;
    let attempt = 0;
    let closed = false;
    let socket: WebSocketLike | null = null;
    let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    function connect(): void {
      if (closed) return;
      socket = factory(streamUrl(deps.baseUrl));
      socket.onopen = () => {
        attempt = 0;
        socket?.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'subscribe', workspaceId, afterSeq: lastSeq }));
      };
      socket.onmessage = (event) => {
        const frame = parseFrame(event.data);
        if (frame === null) return;
        if (frame.type === 'ping') {
          socket?.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'pong' }));
          return;
        }
        if (frame.type === 'event') lastSeq = Math.max(lastSeq, frame.envelope.seq);
        if (frame.type === 'caught-up') lastSeq = Math.max(lastSeq, frame.seq);
        cb(frame);
      };
      socket.onclose = () => {
        socket = null;
        if (closed) return;
        retryTimer = setTimeout(connect, backoffDelayMs(attempt, random));
        attempt += 1;
      };
      socket.onerror = () => {
        socket?.close();
      };
    }

    connect();
    return () => {
      closed = true;
      if (retryTimer !== null) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
      socket?.close();
    };
  };
}
```

```ts
// packages/app-core/src/views/rollup-text.ts
import type { RollupToViewer } from '../view-types.js';

/**
 * "5 of 8 done · 2 restricted" — counts are over ALL direct children
 * (server-computed with no visibility filtering); the children this viewer
 * cannot see are named in the restricted count, never pretended absent
 * (spec §6: "true counts, restriction named, nothing pretended absent").
 */
export function formatRollup(rollup: RollupToViewer): string {
  const base = `${rollup.terminalChildren} of ${rollup.totalChildren} done`;
  return rollup.restrictedCount > 0 ? `${base} · ${rollup.restrictedCount} restricted` : base;
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/use-live-query.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';

export type LiveQueryResult<T> = { data: T | null; error: string | null; reload: () => void };

/**
 * Load once, then refetch (debounced) whenever the workspace stream moves.
 * Deliberately refetch-based: the stream is an invalidation signal, and the
 * projections remain the one source of rendered truth — the client never
 * patches views from raw events (that is Plan 10's replica, built on
 * reducers, not this).
 */
export function useLiveQuery<T>(
  source: DataSource,
  workspaceId: string,
  load: () => Promise<T>,
  debounceMs = 200,
): LiveQueryResult<T> {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const loadRef = useRef(load);
  loadRef.current = load;

  const reload = useCallback(() => {
    loadRef.current().then(
      (value) => {
        setData(value);
        setError(null);
      },
      (cause: unknown) => setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)),
    );
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    reload();
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    const unsubscribe = source.subscribe(workspaceId, 0, (item) => {
      if (item.type !== 'event' && item.type !== 'purge') return;
      if (timer !== null) clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(reload, debounceMs);
    });
    return () => {
      if (timer !== null) clearTimeout(timer);
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [source, workspaceId, debounceMs, reload]);

  return { data, error, reload };
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/board.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Avatar, Card, Chip } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import { actorLabel, formatStateProvenance } from '../provenance.js';
import type { BoardCard as BoardCardData, BoardView as BoardViewData } from '../view-types.js';
import { formatRollup } from './rollup-text.js';
import { useLiveQuery, type LiveQueryResult } from './use-live-query.js';

export type BoardProps = {
  board: BoardViewData;
  onOpenTicket: (ticketId: string) => void;
};

/** Columns are exactly the attached workflow's states — empty ones render too,
 * and a project mixing workflows gets one group per workflow, because merging
 * column sets would invent columns no definition declares (spec §7). */
export function Board({ board, onOpenTicket }: BoardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="gn-board">
      {board.groups.map((group) => (
        <section key={`${group.workflowId}@${group.workflowVersion}`} className="gn-board__group">
          {board.groups.length > 1 ? <h3 className="gn-board__workflow">{group.workflowName}</h3> : null}
          <div className="gn-board__columns">
            {group.columns.map((column) => (
              <section
                key={column.stateId}
                role="region"
                aria-label={column.name}
                className={
                  column.blocked ? 'gn-board__column gn-board__column--blocked' : 'gn-board__column'
                }
              >
                <header className="gn-board__column-header">
                  <Chip tone={column.blocked ? 'blocked' : column.category}>{column.name}</Chip>
                  <span className="gn-board__count">{column.cards.length}</span>
                </header>
                <ul className="gn-board__cards">
                  {column.cards.map((card) => (
                    <li key={card.ticketId} className="gn-board__card-slot">
                      <BoardCardView card={card} stateName={column.name} onOpen={() => onOpenTicket(card.ticketId)} />
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </section>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function BoardCardView({
  card,
  stateName,
  onOpen,
}: {
  card: BoardCardData;
  stateName: string;
  onOpen: () => void;
}) {
  const [unfolded, setUnfolded] = useState(false);
  return (
    <Card className="gn-board-card">
      <button type="button" className="gn-board-card__title" onClick={onOpen}>
        {card.title}
      </button>
      <div className="gn-board-card__meta">
        {card.assignee !== null ? (
          <Avatar name={actorLabel(card.assignee.id, card.assignee.name)} size="small" />
        ) : null}
        {card.pending ? <Chip tone="pending">claim in transit</Chip> : null}
        {card.cause !== null ? (
          <Chip tone="active" title="This parent was reopened because it acquired live work">
            reopened — live work added
          </Chip>
        ) : null}
        {card.rollup !== null ? (
          <span className="gn-board-card__progress">{formatRollup(card.rollup)}</span>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="gn-board-card__provenance"
        aria-expanded={unfolded}
        onClick={() => setUnfolded((value) => !value)}
      >
        {unfolded ? formatStateProvenance(stateName, card.stateProvenance) : 'who · when'}
      </button>
    </Card>
  );
}

export function useBoard(
  source: DataSource,
  workspaceId: string,
  projectId: string,
): LiveQueryResult<BoardViewData> {
  return useLiveQuery(source, workspaceId, () => source.query.getBoard(projectId));
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Grouping } from '../../src/view-types.js';
import {
  countRows,
  groupRows,
  nestRows,
  recentlyAssignedRows,
  visibleNodes,
  type GroupLabels,
} from '../../src/views/list-model.js';
import { claim, listRowSeed } from '../helpers/seed.js';

const LABELS: GroupLabels = {
  epicTitle: (epicId) => (epicId === 'epic_reg' ? 'Registrations' : epicId),
  typeName: (typeId) => typeId,
};

const ROWS = [
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 'epicA', title: 'Registrations', typeId: 'epic', typeRank: 1, epicId: null }),
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 't1', title: 'GST', parentId: 'epicA', epicId: 'epic_reg', stateId: 'done', stateName: 'Done', stateCategory: 'terminal' }),
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 't2', title: 'DLT', parentId: 'epicA', epicId: 'epic_reg', stateId: 'doing', stateName: 'Doing', stateCategory: 'active', assignee: { id: 'me', name: 'Kiro' }, assignedProvenance: claim({ serverTime: '2026-08-19T11:00:00.000Z' }) }),
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 't3', title: 'Sub-task of DLT', parentId: 't2', epicId: 'epic_reg', stateCategory: 'open' }),
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 't4', title: 'Loose task', assignee: { id: 'me', name: 'Kiro' }, assignedProvenance: claim({ serverTime: '2026-08-20T07:00:00.000Z' }) }),
];

const GROUPINGS: Grouping[] = ['epic', 'status', 'assignee', 'type', 'flat'];

describe('grouping arranges truth; it never changes it (spec §7 List)', () => {
  it('PLANTED DEFECT: every grouping renders the same rows and the same counts', () => {
    const baseline = countRows(ROWS);
    for (const grouping of GROUPINGS) {
      const sections = groupRows(ROWS, grouping, LABELS);
      const regrouped = sections.flatMap((section) => section.rows);
      expect(regrouped.map((row) => row.ticketId).sort(), `grouping=${grouping}`).toEqual(
        ROWS.map((row) => row.ticketId).sort(),
      );
      expect(countRows(regrouped), `grouping=${grouping}`).toEqual(baseline);
    }
  });

  it('labels sections honestly: No epic / Unassigned buckets exist rather than vanishing rows', () => {
    const epicSections = groupRows(ROWS, 'epic', LABELS);
    expect(epicSections.map((section) => section.label)).toContain('No epic');
    const assigneeSections = groupRows(ROWS, 'assignee', LABELS);
    expect(assigneeSections.map((section) => section.label)).toContain('Unassigned');
  });
});

describe('nestRows — hierarchy as indentation', () => {
  it('orders parent-before-children with depths, orphans at the root', () => {
    const nodes = nestRows(ROWS);
    const byId = Object.fromEntries(nodes.map((node) => [node.row.ticketId, node]));
    expect(byId.epicA.depth).toBe(0);
    expect(byId.t2.depth).toBe(1);
    expect(byId.t3.depth).toBe(2);
    expect(byId.t4.depth).toBe(0);
    expect(byId.t2.hasChildren).toBe(true);
    expect(byId.t1.hasChildren).toBe(false);
    const order = nodes.map((node) => node.row.ticketId);
    expect(order.indexOf('t2')).toBeLessThan(order.indexOf('t3'));
  });

  it('a row whose parent is absent from the set renders at depth 0 — never dropped', () => {
    const nodes = nestRows([listRowSeed({ ticketId: 'lonely', parentId: 'not-here' })]);
    expect(nodes).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(nodes[0].depth).toBe(0);
  });

  it('visibleNodes hides collapsed subtrees only', () => {
    const nodes = nestRows(ROWS);
    const visible = visibleNodes(nodes, new Set(['t2']));
    const ids = visible.map((node) => node.row.ticketId);
    expect(ids).toContain('t2');
    expect(ids).not.toContain('t3');
    expect(ids).toContain('t1');
  });
});

describe('recentlyAssignedRows — pinned section source', () => {
  it('holds only my assignments, newest claim first', () => {
    const rows = recentlyAssignedRows(ROWS, 'me');
    expect(rows.map((row) => row.ticketId)).toEqual(['t4', 't2']);
  });
});
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/list-model.ts`:

```ts
import { actorLabel } from '../provenance.js';
import type { Grouping, ListColumnId, ListRow } from '../view-types.js';

export type ListSection = { key: string; label: string; rows: ListRow[] };
export type ListRowNode = { row: ListRow; depth: number; hasChildren: boolean };
export type GroupLabels = { epicTitle(epicId: string): string; typeName(typeId: string): string };

/** Counts always derive from the FLAT row set — sections can never change them. */
export function countRows(rows: ListRow[]): { total: number; terminal: number } {
  return {
    total: rows.length,
    terminal: rows.filter((row) => row.stateCategory === 'terminal').length,
  };
}

export function recentlyAssignedRows(rows: ListRow[], currentUserId: string): ListRow[] {
  return rows
    .filter((row) => row.assignee?.id === currentUserId && row.assignedProvenance !== null)
    .sort((first, second) =>
      first.assignedProvenance!.serverTime < second.assignedProvenance!.serverTime ? 1 : -1,
    );
}

/**
 * Pure partition: every row lands in exactly one section, so re-flattening the
 * sections reproduces the input set — the property the planted-defect test
 * holds against every grouping. Absence gets an honest bucket ("No epic",
 * "Unassigned"), never a vanished row.
 */
export function groupRows(rows: ListRow[], grouping: Grouping, labels: GroupLabels): ListSection[] {
  if (grouping === 'flat') return [{ key: 'all', label: 'All tickets', rows: [...rows] }];
  const keyAndLabel = (row: ListRow): [string, string] => {
    switch (grouping) {
      case 'epic':
        return row.epicId === null ? ['epic:none', 'No epic'] : [`epic:${row.epicId}`, labels.epicTitle(row.epicId)];
      case 'status':
        return [`status:${row.stateId}`, row.stateName];
      case 'assignee':
        return row.assignee === null
          ? ['assignee:none', 'Unassigned']
          : [`assignee:${row.assignee.id}`, actorLabel(row.assignee.id, row.assignee.name)];
      case 'type':
        return [`type:${row.typeId}`, labels.typeName(row.typeId)];
    }
  };
  const sections = new Map<string, ListSection>();
  for (const row of rows) {
    const [key, label] = keyAndLabel(row);
    const section = sections.get(key) ?? { key, label, rows: [] };
    section.rows.push(row);
    sections.set(key, section);
  }
  return [...sections.values()];
}

/** Depth-first nesting; a row whose parent is outside the set roots at depth 0 — never dropped. */
export function nestRows(rows: ListRow[]): ListRowNode[] {
  const ids = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.ticketId));
  const byParent = new Map<string | null, ListRow[]>();
  for (const row of rows) {
    const key = row.parentId !== null && ids.has(row.parentId) ? row.parentId : null;
    const bucket = byParent.get(key) ?? [];
    bucket.push(row);
    byParent.set(key, bucket);
  }
  const nodes: ListRowNode[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<string>();
  const visit = (parentKey: string | null, depth: number): void => {
    for (const row of byParent.get(parentKey) ?? []) {
      if (visited.has(row.ticketId)) continue; // defensive: the engine refuses cycles, replay must not loop
      visited.add(row.ticketId);
      const children = byParent.get(row.ticketId) ?? [];
      nodes.push({ row, depth, hasChildren: children.length > 0 });
      visit(row.ticketId, depth + 1);
    }
  };
  visit(null, 0);
  return nodes;
}

export function visibleNodes(nodes: ListRowNode[], collapsed: ReadonlySet<string>): ListRowNode[] {
  const visible: ListRowNode[] = [];
  let hiddenBelowDepth: number | null = null;
  for (const node of nodes) {
    if (hiddenBelowDepth !== null && node.depth > hiddenBelowDepth) continue;
    hiddenBelowDepth = null;
    visible.push(node);
    if (collapsed.has(node.row.ticketId)) hiddenBelowDepth = node.depth;
  }
  return visible;
}

const BUILTIN_COLUMN_LABELS: Record<Exclude<ListColumnId, `field:${string}`>, string> = {
  status: 'Status',
  assignee: 'Assignee',
  dueDate: 'Due date',
  type: 'Type',
  progress: 'Progress',
};

export function columnLabel(id: ListColumnId): string {
  if (id.startsWith('field:')) return id.slice('field:'.length);
  return BUILTIN_COLUMN_LABELS[id as Exclude<ListColumnId, `field:${string}`>];
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { act } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS, type UserPreferences } from '../../src/view-types.js';
import { TicketList, useTicketList } from '../../src/views/ticket-list.js';
import type { GroupLabels } from '../../src/views/list-model.js';
import { createFakeDataSource } from '../helpers/fake-data-source.js';
import { WS, claim, listRowSeed } from '../helpers/seed.js';

const LABELS: GroupLabels = { epicTitle: (id) => (id === 'epic_reg' ? 'Registrations' : id), typeName: (id) => id };

const ROWS = [
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 'epicA', title: 'Registrations', typeId: 'epic', typeRank: 1 }),
  listRowSeed({
    ticketId: 't2',
    title: 'DLT registration',
    parentId: 'epicA',
    epicId: 'epic_reg',
    stateId: 'doing',
    stateName: 'Doing',
    stateCategory: 'active',
    assignee: { id: 'me', name: 'Kiro' },
    assignedProvenance: claim(),
    dueDate: { date: '2026-08-30', claim: claim({ actorName: 'Kiro', actorId: 'me' }) },
  }),
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 't3', title: 'Chase the vendor', parentId: 't2', epicId: 'epic_reg' }),
  listRowSeed({ ticketId: 't5', title: 'Done thing', stateId: 'done', stateName: 'Done', stateCategory: 'terminal' }),
];

function renderList(prefs = DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS, onPrefsChange = vi.fn()) {
  render(
    <TicketList
      rows={ROWS}
      currentUserId="me"
      prefs={prefs}
      onPrefsChange={onPrefsChange}
      labels={LABELS}
      onOpenTicket={vi.fn()}
    />,
  );
  return onPrefsChange;
}

describe('TicketList — Asana-shaped, honest to the row set', () => {
  it('pins Recently assigned at the top with my newest assignment', () => {
    renderList();
    const pinned = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recently assigned' });
    expect(within(pinned).getByText('DLT registration')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('PLANTED DEFECT: the summary line is identical under every grouping — arranging truth never changes it', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderList();
    const summary = () => screen.getByTestId('list-summary').textContent;
    const baseline = summary();
    expect(baseline).toBe('4 tickets · 1 done');
    for (const grouping of ['status', 'assignee', 'type', 'flat', 'epic']) {
      await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Group by'), grouping);
      expect(summary(), `grouping=${grouping}`).toBe(baseline);
    }
  });

  it('renders chosen columns in chosen order, including the attributed due-date claim', () => {
    renderList({ columns: ['dueDate', 'status'], grouping: 'flat' });
    const headers = screen.getAllByRole('columnheader').map((header) => header.textContent);
    expect(headers).toEqual(['Title', 'Due date', 'Status']);
    expect(screen.getByText("30 Aug 2026 — Kiro's claim")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('collapses a subtree without touching the summary counts', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderList({ columns: ['status'], grouping: 'flat' });
    expect(screen.getByText('Chase the vendor')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Collapse DLT registration' }));
    expect(screen.queryByText('Chase the vendor')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('list-summary')).toHaveTextContent('4 tickets · 1 done');
  });

  it('persists grouping and column changes through onPrefsChange', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onPrefsChange = renderList();
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Group by'), 'status');
    expect(onPrefsChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ ...DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS, grouping: 'status' });
  });
});

describe('useTicketList — preferences round-trip through the DataSource (amendment 14)', () => {
  function Harness({ source }: { source: ReturnType<typeof createFakeDataSource> }) {
    const list = useTicketList(source, WS, 'prj_1', 'me');
    if (list.rows === null) return <div>loading</div>;
    return (
      <button type="button" onClick={() => list.setPrefs({ columns: ['status'], grouping: 'flat' })}>
        prefs:{list.prefs.grouping}
      </button>
    );
  }

  it('loads stored prefs and writes changes back via putPreferences', async () => {
    const stored: UserPreferences = {
      version: 1,
      projects: { prj_1: { columns: ['assignee'], grouping: 'type' } },
      savedFilters: [],
    };
    const putPreferences = vi.fn(async () => {});
    const source = createFakeDataSource({
      getTickets: async () => ({ projectId: 'prj_1', rows: ROWS }),
      getPreferences: async () => stored,
      putPreferences,
    });
    render(<Harness source={source} />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText('prefs:type')).toBeInTheDocument());
    await act(async () => {
      screen.getByRole('button').click();
    });
    expect(putPreferences).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      version: 1,
      projects: { prj_1: { columns: ['status'], grouping: 'flat' } },
      savedFilters: [],
    });
  });
});
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/ticket-list.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Avatar, Chip } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import { actorLabel, formatDate } from '../provenance.js';
import {
  DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS,
  type Grouping,
  type ListColumnId,
  type ListRow,
  type ProjectViewPrefs,
  type UserPreferences,
} from '../view-types.js';
import {
  columnLabel,
  countRows,
  groupRows,
  nestRows,
  recentlyAssignedRows,
  visibleNodes,
  type GroupLabels,
  type ListRowNode,
} from './list-model.js';
import { formatRollup } from './rollup-text.js';
import { useLiveQuery } from './use-live-query.js';

const GROUPING_OPTIONS: Grouping[] = ['epic', 'status', 'assignee', 'type', 'flat'];
const AVAILABLE_COLUMNS: ListColumnId[] = ['status', 'assignee', 'dueDate', 'type', 'progress'];

export type TicketListProps = {
  rows: ListRow[];
  currentUserId: string;
  prefs: ProjectViewPrefs;
  onPrefsChange: (prefs: ProjectViewPrefs) => void;
  labels: GroupLabels;
  onOpenTicket: (ticketId: string) => void;
};

export function TicketList({ rows, currentUserId, prefs, onPrefsChange, labels, onOpenTicket }: TicketListProps) {
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const counts = countRows(rows); // ALWAYS from the flat set — sections cannot alter it
  const sections = useMemo(() => groupRows(rows, prefs.grouping, labels), [rows, prefs.grouping, labels]);
  const pinned = useMemo(() => recentlyAssignedRows(rows, currentUserId), [rows, currentUserId]);

  const toggleCollapse = (ticketId: string) => {
    setCollapsed((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(ticketId)) next.delete(ticketId);
      else next.add(ticketId);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const toggleColumn = (id: ListColumnId) => {
    const columns = prefs.columns.includes(id)
      ? prefs.columns.filter((column) => column !== id)
      : [...prefs.columns, id];
    onPrefsChange({ ...prefs, columns });
  };

  return (
    <div className="gn-list">
      <div className="gn-list__controls">
        <span data-testid="list-summary" className="gn-list__summary">
          {counts.total} tickets · {counts.terminal} done
        </span>
        <label className="gn-list__control">
          Group by
          <select
            value={prefs.grouping}
            onChange={(event) => onPrefsChange({ ...prefs, grouping: event.target.value as Grouping })}
          >
            {GROUPING_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
              <option key={option} value={option}>
                {option}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <details className="gn-list__columns-picker">
          <summary>Columns</summary>
          {AVAILABLE_COLUMNS.map((id) => (
            <label key={id} className="gn-list__column-option">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={prefs.columns.includes(id)} onChange={() => toggleColumn(id)} />
              {columnLabel(id)}
            </label>
          ))}
        </details>
      </div>

      {pinned.length > 0 ? (
        <section role="region" aria-label="Recently assigned" className="gn-list__pinned">
          <h3 className="gn-list__section-title">Recently assigned</h3>
          <ListTable
            nodes={pinned.map((row) => ({ row, depth: 0, hasChildren: false }))}
            columns={prefs.columns}
            collapsed={collapsed}
            onToggleCollapse={toggleCollapse}
            onOpenTicket={onOpenTicket}
          />
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {sections.map((section) => {
        const nodes = visibleNodes(nestRows(section.rows), collapsed);
        return (
          <section key={section.key} role="region" aria-label={section.label} className="gn-list__section">
            {prefs.grouping === 'flat' ? null : <h3 className="gn-list__section-title">{section.label}</h3>}
            <ListTable
              nodes={nodes}
              columns={prefs.columns}
              collapsed={collapsed}
              onToggleCollapse={toggleCollapse}
              onOpenTicket={onOpenTicket}
            />
          </section>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function ListTable({
  nodes,
  columns,
  collapsed,
  onToggleCollapse,
  onOpenTicket,
}: {
  nodes: ListRowNode[];
  columns: ListColumnId[];
  collapsed: ReadonlySet<string>;
  onToggleCollapse: (ticketId: string) => void;
  onOpenTicket: (ticketId: string) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="gn-table__scroll">
      <table className="gn-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col" className="gn-table__header">
              Title
            </th>
            {columns.map((id) => (
              <th key={id} scope="col" className="gn-table__header">
                {columnLabel(id)}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {nodes.map((node) => (
            <tr key={node.row.ticketId} className="gn-table__row">
              <td className="gn-table__cell">
                <span className="gn-list__title-cell" style={{ paddingLeft: `${node.depth * 1.1}rem` }}>
                  {node.hasChildren ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="gn-list__collapse"
                      aria-label={`${collapsed.has(node.row.ticketId) ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse'} ${node.row.title}`}
                      onClick={() => onToggleCollapse(node.row.ticketId)}
                    >
                      {collapsed.has(node.row.ticketId) ? '▸' : '▾'}
                    </button>
                  ) : null}
                  <button type="button" className="gn-list__open" onClick={() => onOpenTicket(node.row.ticketId)}>
                    {node.row.title}
                  </button>
                </span>
              </td>
              {columns.map((id) => (
                <td key={id} className="gn-table__cell">
                  <ListCell row={node.row} columnId={id} />
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

function ListCell({ row, columnId }: { row: ListRow; columnId: ListColumnId }) {
  switch (columnId) {
    case 'status':
      return (
        <Chip
          tone={row.stateCategory}
          title={`marked ${row.stateName} by ${actorLabel(row.stateProvenance.actorId, row.stateProvenance.actorName)} on ${formatDate(row.stateProvenance.serverTime)}`}
        >
          {row.stateName}
        </Chip>
      );
    case 'assignee':
      return row.assignee === null ? (
        <span className="gn-list__empty-cell">—</span>
      ) : (
        <span className="gn-list__assignee">
          <Avatar name={actorLabel(row.assignee.id, row.assignee.name)} size="small" />
          {actorLabel(row.assignee.id, row.assignee.name)}
        </span>
      );
    case 'dueDate':
      // A date is a claim and renders attributed (spec §7 Calendar's rule, applied here too).
      return row.dueDate === null ? (
        <span className="gn-list__empty-cell">—</span>
      ) : (
        <span>
          {formatDate(row.dueDate.date)} — {actorLabel(row.dueDate.claim.actorId, row.dueDate.claim.actorName)}'s claim
        </span>
      );
    case 'type':
      return <span>{row.typeId}</span>;
    case 'progress':
      return row.rollup === null ? <span className="gn-list__empty-cell">—</span> : <span>{formatRollup(row.rollup)}</span>;
    default: {
      const fieldId = columnId.slice('field:'.length);
      const fieldClaim = row.fields[fieldId];
      if (fieldClaim === undefined) return <span className="gn-list__empty-cell">—</span>;
      return (
        <span title={`set by ${actorLabel(fieldClaim.actorId, null)} on ${formatDate(fieldClaim.serverTime)}`}>
          {String(fieldClaim.value)}
        </span>
      );
    }
  }
}

export function useTicketList(source: DataSource, workspaceId: string, projectId: string, currentUserId: string) {
  const list = useLiveQuery(source, workspaceId, () => source.query.getTickets(projectId, {}, 'flat'));
  const preferences = useLiveQuery(source, workspaceId, () => source.query.getPreferences());
  const stored = preferences.data?.projects[projectId] ?? DEFAULT_VIEW_PREFS;
  const setPrefs = (next: ProjectViewPrefs) => {
    const document: UserPreferences = {
      version: 1,
      projects: { ...(preferences.data?.projects ?? {}), [projectId]: next },
      savedFilters: preferences.data?.savedFilters ?? [],
    };
    void source.query.putPreferences(document).then(() => preferences.reload());
  };
  return {
    rows: list.data?.rows ?? null,
    prefs: stored,
    setPrefs,
    currentUserId,
    error: list.error ?? preferences.error,
    reload: list.reload,
  };
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { AttentionItem } from '../../src/view-types.js';
import { AttentionStrip } from '../../src/views/attention-strip.js';

const ITEMS: AttentionItem[] = [
  {
    kind: 'blocked',
    ticketId: 't1',
    projectId: 'prj_1',
    title: 'DLT registration',
    reason: 'In "Waiting on counterparty" for 12 days',
    claim: claim(),
  },
  {
    kind: 'due-soon',
    ticketId: 't2',
    projectId: 'prj_1',
    title: 'File the GST return',
    reason: 'Needed by 30 Aug — 3 days away',
    claim: claim({ actorName: null, actorId: 'user_kiro' }),
  },
  {
    kind: 'contested',
    ticketId: 't3',
    projectId: 'prj_1',
    title: 'Bank mandate',
    reason: 'Refused on sync: restrict-to — only the assignee may close this',
    claim: claim(),
  },
  {
    kind: 'stale',
    ticketId: 't4',
    projectId: 'prj_1',
    title: 'Old errand',
    reason: 'Active for 40 days with no movement',
    claim: null,
  },
];

describe('AttentionStrip — computed, never authored (spec §7)', () => {
  it('renders every item with its kind and its computed reason', () => {
    render(<AttentionStrip items={ITEMS} onOpenTicket={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('In "Waiting on counterparty" for 12 days')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Needed by 30 Aug — 3 days away')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Refused on sync: restrict-to — only the assignee may close this')).toBeInTheDocument();

  });

    render(<AttentionStrip items={[ITEMS[0]]} onOpenTicket={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('claimed by Anselma Marak on 19 Aug 2026')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('PLANTED DEFECT: a nameless claim is attributed by id, and a claimless item invents nothing', () => {
    render(<AttentionStrip items={[ITEMS[1], ITEMS[3]]} onOpenTicket={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('claimed by user_kiro on 19 Aug 2026')).toBeInTheDocument();
    // The stale item carries no claim: exactly one provenance line total.
    expect(screen.getAllByText(/^claimed by/)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('renders nothing at all when there is nothing needing attention — no fabricated urgency', () => {
    const { container } = render(<AttentionStrip items={[]} onOpenTicket={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
  });
});
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/attention-strip.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Chip, type ChipTone } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import { actorLabel, formatDate } from '../provenance.js';
import type { AttentionItem, AttentionKind } from '../view-types.js';
import { useLiveQuery, type LiveQueryResult } from './use-live-query.js';

const KIND_TONE: Record<AttentionKind, ChipTone> = {
  blocked: 'blocked',
  stale: 'pending',
  'due-soon': 'pending',
  contested: 'contested',
};

const KIND_LABEL: Record<AttentionKind, string> = {
  blocked: 'Blocked',
  stale: 'Stale',
  'due-soon': 'Due soon',
  contested: 'Contested',
};

export type AttentionStripProps = {
  items: AttentionItem[];
  onOpenTicket: (ticketId: string) => void;
};

/**
 * Computed, never authored (spec §7). Every entry names its reason; when the
 * entry rests on a claim (a due date, a contested edit), that claim's
 * provenance renders with it — and when it does not, nothing is invented.
 */
export function AttentionStrip({ items, onOpenTicket }: AttentionStripProps) {
  if (items.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <aside className="gn-attention" aria-label="Needs attention">
      <ul className="gn-attention__items">
        {items.map((item) => (
          <li key={`${item.kind}:${item.ticketId}`} className="gn-attention__item">
            <Chip tone={KIND_TONE[item.kind]}>{KIND_LABEL[item.kind]}</Chip>
            <button type="button" className="gn-attention__title" onClick={() => onOpenTicket(item.ticketId)}>
              {item.title}
            </button>
            <span className="gn-attention__reason">{item.reason}</span>
            {item.claim !== null ? (
              <span className="gn-attention__claim">
                claimed by {actorLabel(item.claim.actorId, item.claim.actorName)} on {formatDate(item.claim.serverTime)}
              </span>
            ) : null}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </aside>
  );
}

export function useAttention(source: DataSource, workspaceId: string): LiveQueryResult<AttentionItem[]> {
  return useLiveQuery(source, workspaceId, () => source.query.getAttention(workspaceId));
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/my-work.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Card, Chip } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import { actorLabel, formatDate, formatStateProvenance } from '../provenance.js';
import type { MyWorkView, WorkItem } from '../view-types.js';
import { useLiveQuery, type LiveQueryResult } from './use-live-query.js';

export type MyWorkProps = {
  view: MyWorkView;
  onOpenTicket: (ticketId: string) => void;
};

export function MyWork({ view, onOpenTicket }: MyWorkProps) {
  return (
    <div className="gn-my-work">
      <section role="region" aria-label="Assigned to me" className="gn-my-work__section">
        <h3 className="gn-my-work__heading">Assigned to me</h3>
        {view.assigned.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="gn-my-work__empty">Nothing assigned to you.</p>
        ) : (
          view.assigned.map((item) => <WorkItemCard key={item.ticketId} item={item} onOpenTicket={onOpenTicket} />)
        )}
      </section>

      <section role="region" aria-label="My claims in transit" className="gn-my-work__section">
        <h3 className="gn-my-work__heading">My claims in transit</h3>
        {view.inTransit.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="gn-my-work__empty">No claims in transit.</p>
        ) : (
          view.inTransit.map((item) => (
            <WorkItemCard key={item.ticketId} item={item} onOpenTicket={onOpenTicket} pending />
          ))
        )}
      </section>

      <section role="region" aria-label="My contested claims" className="gn-my-work__section">
        <h3 className="gn-my-work__heading">My contested claims</h3>
        {view.contested.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="gn-my-work__empty">No contested claims.</p>
        ) : (
          view.contested.map((contested) => (
            <Card key={contested.ticketId} className="gn-my-work__contested">
              <button type="button" className="gn-my-work__title" onClick={() => onOpenTicket(contested.ticketId)}>
                {contested.title}
              </button>
              <Chip tone="contested">{contested.rule}</Chip>
              <p className="gn-my-work__reason">{contested.reason}</p>
              <span className="gn-my-work__claim">
                claimed by {actorLabel(contested.claim.actorId, contested.claim.actorName)} on{' '}
                {formatDate(contested.claim.serverTime)}
              </span>
            </Card>
          ))
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function WorkItemCard({
  item,
  onOpenTicket,
  pending = false,
}: {
  item: WorkItem;
  onOpenTicket: (ticketId: string) => void;
  pending?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <Card className="gn-my-work__item">
      <button type="button" className="gn-my-work__title" onClick={() => onOpenTicket(item.ticketId)}>
        {item.title}
      </button>
      <span className="gn-my-work__project">{item.projectName}</span>
      <Chip tone={item.stateCategory} title={formatStateProvenance(item.stateName, item.stateProvenance)}>
        {item.stateName}
      </Chip>
      {pending ? <Chip tone="pending">claim in transit</Chip> : null}
      {item.dueDate !== null ? (
        <span className="gn-my-work__due">
          needed by {formatDate(item.dueDate.date)} — {actorLabel(item.dueDate.claim.actorId, item.dueDate.claim.actorName)}
          's claim
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </Card>
  );
}

export function useMyWork(source: DataSource, workspaceId: string): LiveQueryResult<MyWorkView> {
  return useLiveQuery(source, workspaceId, () => source.query.getMyWork());
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/editor-slot.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { docToPlainText } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';

/**
 * The seam Plan 7 fills with the real Tiptap editor (amendment 12). This plan
 * ships the read-only default; the props contract is pinned here and Plan 7
 * implements the SAME props, so no mount point moves when it lands.
 */
export type EditorSlotProps = {
  doc: unknown;
  readOnly: boolean;
  workspaceId: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  autoFocus?: boolean;
  onSubmit?: (doc: unknown) => void;
};

export type EditorSlotComponent = (props: EditorSlotProps) => ReactElement | null;

/** Read-only default: an honest plain-text projection of the document. */
export function PlainTextEditorSlot({ doc, placeholder }: EditorSlotProps): ReactElement {
  const text = docToPlainText(doc);
  return (
    <div className="gn-editor-slot gn-editor-slot--plain">
      {text.length > 0 ? text : <span className="gn-editor-slot__placeholder">{placeholder ?? ''}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/history-list.tsx`:

```tsx
import { formatDateTime } from '../provenance.js';
import type { TicketHistoryEvent } from '../view-types.js';

/**
 * The history view IS the ledger, filtered (spec §3). Every entry shows the
 * actor, the origin device, and BOTH clocks with their honest labels — the
 * user's claim about when, and the system's record of when. Nothing is
 * summarised into one "time".
 */
export function HistoryList({
  events,
  nameOf,
}: {
  events: TicketHistoryEvent[];
  nameOf: (userId: string) => string;
}) {
  return (
    <ol className="gn-history">
      {events.map((event) => (
        <li key={event.id} className="gn-history__event">
          <div className="gn-history__headline">
            <span className="gn-history__type">{event.type}</span>
            <span className="gn-history__actor">{nameOf(event.actorId)}</span>
            <span className="gn-history__origin">from {event.origin}</span>
          </div>
          <dl className="gn-history__clocks">
            <dt>claimed (device clock)</dt>
            <dd>{formatDateTime(event.clientTime)}</dd>
            <dt>recorded (server)</dt>
            <dd>{formatDateTime(event.serverTime)}</dd>
          </dl>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `transition-dialog.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { WorkflowDef } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import { Button, Dialog } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { FieldClaim } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { PrecheckResult } from '../precheck.js';

type TransitionRules = WorkflowDef['transitions'][number]['rules'];

export type TransitionOption = { toStateId: string; name: string; rules: TransitionRules };

/** Only what the definition declares — never an invented move (spec §7). */
export function transitionOptions(def: WorkflowDef, fromStateId: string): TransitionOption[] {
  const targets = new Map<string, TransitionRules>();
  for (const transition of def.transitions) {
    if (transition.from !== fromStateId && transition.from !== '*') continue;
    if (transition.to === fromStateId) continue;
    if (!targets.has(transition.to)) targets.set(transition.to, transition.rules);
  }
  return [...targets.entries()]
    .map(([toStateId, rules]) => {
      const state = def.states.find((candidate) => candidate.id === toStateId);
      return state === undefined ? null : { toStateId, name: state.name, rules };
    })
    .filter((option): option is TransitionOption => option !== null);
}

export type TransitionSubmission = {
  toStateId: string;
  comment?: string;
  evidenceAttachmentId?: string;
  fieldValues: Record<string, unknown>;
};

export type TransitionDialogProps = {
  open: boolean;
  workflow: WorkflowDef;
  fromStateId: string;
  ticketFields: Record<string, FieldClaim>;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (submission: TransitionSubmission) => void;
  /** Instant named-reason preview, advisory only (Task 6's precheckEvent, wired by the sheet). */
  precheck: (toStateId: string) => Promise<PrecheckResult>;
  /** The authority's answer, shown VERBATIM whenever present. */
  serverRefusal: { rule: string; reason: string } | null;
  uploadEvidence?: (file: File) => Promise<{ attachmentId: string }>;
};

export function TransitionDialog({
  open,
  workflow,
  fromStateId,
  ticketFields,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
  precheck,
  serverRefusal,
  uploadEvidence,
}: TransitionDialogProps) {
  const options = transitionOptions(workflow, fromStateId);
  const [toStateId, setToStateId] = useState('');
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('');
  const [fieldValues, setFieldValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [evidenceAttachmentId, setEvidenceAttachmentId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<PrecheckResult | null>(null);

  const selected = options.find((option) => option.toStateId === toStateId) ?? null;
  const rules = selected?.rules ?? [];
  const needsComment = rules.some((rule) => rule.kind === 'require-comment');
  const needsEvidence = rules.some((rule) => rule.kind === 'require-evidence');
  const requiredFields = rules.flatMap((rule) => (rule.kind === 'require-field' ? [rule.field] : []));
  const missingFields = requiredFields.filter(
    (field) => ticketFields[field]?.value == null && (fieldValues[field] ?? '') === '',
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selected === null) {
      setPreview(null);
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    precheck(selected.toStateId).then((result) => {
      if (!cancelled) setPreview(result);
    });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [selected, precheck]);

  const blocked =
    selected === null ||
    (needsComment && comment.trim() === '') ||
    (needsEvidence && evidenceAttachmentId === null) ||
    missingFields.length > 0;

  const submit = () => {
    if (selected === null) return;
    const submission: TransitionSubmission = {
      toStateId: selected.toStateId,
      fieldValues: Object.fromEntries(
        requiredFields.filter((field) => (fieldValues[field] ?? '') !== '').map((field) => [field, fieldValues[field]]),
      ),
    };
    if (needsComment) submission.comment = comment.trim();
    if (evidenceAttachmentId !== null) submission.evidenceAttachmentId = evidenceAttachmentId;
    onSubmit(submission);
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} title="Move ticket" onClose={onClose}>
      <label className="gn-transition__field">
        Move to
        <select value={toStateId} onChange={(event) => setToStateId(event.target.value)}>
          <option value="" disabled>
            Choose a state
          </option>
          {options.map((option) => (
            <option key={option.toStateId} value={option.toStateId}>
              {option.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>

      {needsComment ? (
        <label className="gn-transition__field">
          Comment (required by this transition)
          <textarea value={comment} onChange={(event) => setComment(event.target.value)} />
        </label>
      ) : null}

      {requiredFields.map((field) =>
        ticketFields[field]?.value != null ? null : (
          <label key={field} className="gn-transition__field">
            {field} (required before this transition)
            <input
              value={fieldValues[field] ?? ''}
              onChange={(event) => setFieldValues((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))}
            />
          </label>
        ),
      )}

      {needsEvidence ? (
        uploadEvidence !== undefined ? (
          <label className="gn-transition__field">
            Evidence (required by this transition)
            <input
              type="file"
              onChange={(event) => {
                const file = event.target.files?.[0];
                if (file === undefined) return;
                void uploadEvidence(file).then(({ attachmentId }) => setEvidenceAttachmentId(attachmentId));
              }}
            />
          </label>
        ) : (
          <p className="gn-transition__notice">
            This transition requires an attachment as evidence. Evidence upload is not available in this build yet.
          </p>
        )
      ) : null}

      {rules.some((rule) => rule.kind === 'restrict-to' || rule.kind === 'require-different-user') ? (
        <p className="gn-transition__notice">
          This transition carries {rules
            .filter((rule) => rule.kind === 'restrict-to' || rule.kind === 'require-different-user')
            .map((rule) => rule.kind)
            .join(' and ')}{' '}
          rules — the server decides, and its answer appears here.
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {preview !== null && preview.kind === 'refused' ? (
        <p className="gn-refusal gn-refusal--preview">
          instant check — {preview.rule}: {preview.reason}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {serverRefusal !== null ? (
        <p role="alert" className="gn-refusal gn-refusal--server">
          Refused by the server — {serverRefusal.rule}: {serverRefusal.reason}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="gn-transition__actions">
        <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
        <Button variant="primary" disabled={blocked} onClick={submit}>
          Move
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

Run: `pnpm --filter @gisik-nokma/app-core test -- transition-dialog`
Expected: PASS.
