- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `ticket-sheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import { Button, Chip, Sheet } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { ClientStateCache } from '../cache.js';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import { precheckEvent, type PrecheckResult } from '../precheck.js';
import { actorLabel, formatDate, formatStateProvenance } from '../provenance.js';
import { makeSubmittedFactory } from '../submit.js';
import type { TicketHistoryEvent, TicketRead } from '../view-types.js';
import { PlainTextEditorSlot, type EditorSlotComponent } from './editor-slot.js';
import { HistoryList } from './history-list.js';
import { formatRollup } from './rollup-text.js';
import { TransitionDialog, type TransitionSubmission } from './transition-dialog.js';
import { useLiveQuery } from './use-live-query.js';

export type TicketSheetProps = {
  dataSource: DataSource;
  cache?: ClientStateCache;
  workspaceId: string;
  ticketId: string;
  currentUserId: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  editorSlot?: EditorSlotComponent;
  uploadEvidence?: (file: File) => Promise<{ attachmentId: string }>;
};

export function TicketSheet({
  dataSource,
  cache,
  workspaceId,
  ticketId,
  currentUserId,
  onClose,
  editorSlot,
  uploadEvidence,
}: TicketSheetProps) {
  const EditorSlot = editorSlot ?? PlainTextEditorSlot;
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'details' | 'history'>('details');
  const [moveOpen, setMoveOpen] = useState(false);
  const [serverRefusal, setServerRefusal] = useState<{ rule: string; reason: string } | null>(null);

  const read = useLiveQuery(dataSource, workspaceId, () => dataSource.query.getTicket(workspaceId, ticketId));
  const workflow = useLiveQuery(dataSource, workspaceId, async () => {
    const current = await dataSource.query.getTicket(workspaceId, ticketId);
    return current === null ? null : dataSource.query.getWorkflowDef(workspaceId, current.ticket.workflowId);
  });
  const members = useLiveQuery(dataSource, workspaceId, () => dataSource.query.getMembers(workspaceId));
  const history = useLiveQuery(dataSource, workspaceId, () => dataSource.query.getTicketHistory(ticketId));

  if (read.data === null || read.data === undefined) {
    return (
      <Sheet open title="Ticket" onClose={onClose}>
        <p className="gn-ticket-sheet__loading">{read.error ?? 'Loading…'}</p>
      </Sheet>
    );
  }

  const ticketRead: TicketRead = read.data;
  const nameOf = (userId: string): string =>
    actorLabel(userId, members.data?.find((member) => member.id === userId)?.name ?? null);
  const make = makeSubmittedFactory({ actorId: currentUserId, origin: 'web' });

  const precheck = async (toStateId: string): Promise<PrecheckResult> => {
    if (cache === undefined) return { kind: 'not-evaluated', why: 'missing-local-state' };
    const candidate: SubmittedEvent = make({
      workspaceId,
      entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: ticketId },
      type: 'ticket.transitioned',
      payload: { toStateId },
    });
    return precheckEvent(candidate, cache);
  };

  const submitTransition = async (submission: TransitionSubmission): Promise<void> => {
    const fieldEvents = Object.entries(submission.fieldValues).map(([fieldId, value]) =>
      make({ workspaceId, entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: ticketId }, type: 'ticket.field_set', payload: { fieldId, value } }),
    );
    const transitionPayload: Record<string, unknown> = { toStateId: submission.toStateId };
    if (submission.comment !== undefined) transitionPayload.comment = submission.comment;
    if (submission.evidenceAttachmentId !== undefined) transitionPayload.evidenceAttachmentId = submission.evidenceAttachmentId;
    const transition = make({
      workspaceId,
      entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: ticketId },
      type: 'ticket.transitioned',
      payload: transitionPayload,
    });
    const results = await dataSource.ingest([...fieldEvents, transition]);
    const failed = results.find((result) => result.status !== 'committed');
    if (failed === undefined) {
      setServerRefusal(null);
      setMoveOpen(false);
      read.reload();
      history.reload();
      return;
    }
    if (failed.status === 'refused') setServerRefusal({ rule: failed.rule, reason: failed.reason });
    else if (failed.status === 'invalid') setServerRefusal({ rule: failed.code, reason: failed.message });
  };

  const state = ticketRead.ticket;
  const currentStateName =
    workflow.data?.states.find((candidate) => candidate.id === state.stateId)?.name ?? state.stateId;

  return (
    <Sheet open title={ticketRead.title} onClose={onClose}>
      <div role="tablist" className="gn-ticket-sheet__tabs">
        <button role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={tab === 'details'} onClick={() => setTab('details')}>
          Details
        </button>
        <button role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={tab === 'history'} onClick={() => setTab('history')}>
          History
        </button>
      </div>

      {tab === 'details' ? (
        <div className="gn-ticket-sheet__details">
          <div className="gn-ticket-sheet__state">
            <Chip tone={state.stateCategory}>{currentStateName}</Chip>
            <span className="gn-ticket-sheet__provenance">
              {formatStateProvenance(currentStateName, ticketRead.stateProvenance)}
            </span>
            <Button onClick={() => setMoveOpen(true)}>Move…</Button>
          </div>

          <div className="gn-ticket-sheet__parent">
            {ticketRead.parent === null ? (
              <span>At project root</span>
            ) : ticketRead.parent.kind === 'restricted' ? (
              <span className="gn-ticket-sheet__restricted">Restricted parent — exists, but you can't see it</span>
            ) : (
              <span>{ticketRead.parent.title}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          {ticketRead.rollup !== null ? (
            <p className="gn-ticket-sheet__progress">{formatRollup(ticketRead.rollup)}</p>
          ) : null}

          <dl className="gn-ticket-sheet__fields">
            <div className="gn-ticket-sheet__field" data-testid="field-assignee">
              <dt>Assignee</dt>
              <dd>{state.assigneeId === null ? '—' : nameOf(state.assigneeId)}</dd>
            </div>
            {Object.entries(state.fields).map(([fieldId, fieldClaim]) => (
              <div key={fieldId} className="gn-ticket-sheet__field" data-testid={`field-${fieldId}`}>
                <dt>{fieldId}</dt>
                <dd>
                  {fieldId === 'description' ? (
                    <EditorSlot doc={fieldClaim.value} readOnly workspaceId={workspaceId} placeholder="No description yet" />
                  ) : (
                    <span>{renderFieldValue(fieldClaim.value)}</span>
                  )}
                  <span className="gn-ticket-sheet__claim">
                    set by {nameOf(fieldClaim.actorId)} on {formatDate(fieldClaim.serverTime)}
                  </span>
                </dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </dl>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <HistoryList events={history.data ?? ([] as TicketHistoryEvent[])} nameOf={nameOf} />
      )}

      {workflow.data != null ? (
        <TransitionDialog
          open={moveOpen}
          workflow={workflow.data}
          fromStateId={state.stateId}
          ticketFields={state.fields}
          onClose={() => {
            setMoveOpen(false);
            setServerRefusal(null);
          }}
          onSubmit={(submission) => void submitTransition(submission)}
          precheck={precheck}
          serverRefusal={serverRefusal}
          uploadEvidence={uploadEvidence}
        />
      ) : null}
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function renderFieldValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '—';
  if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && 'date' in (value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    return formatDate(String((value as Record<string, unknown>).date));
  }
  return String(value);
}
```

### Task 13: The reopen-option flow — live children under terminal parents (owner decision)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-core/src/flows/guarded-child-event.ts`
- Create: `packages/app-core/src/views/reopen-dialog.tsx`
- Create: `packages/app-core/src/views/new-ticket-dialog.tsx`
- Create: `packages/app-core/src/views/create-ticket-flow.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-core/src/index.ts` (re-exports)
- Test: `packages/app-core/test/flows/guarded-child-event.test.ts`
- Test: `packages/app-core/test/views/reopen-flow.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DataSource`, `IngestResult`, `SubmittedEvent`, `WorkflowDef` (Tasks 4–5); `makeSubmittedFactory`, `precheckEvent`, `PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE` (Task 6); `Dialog`, `Button` (`@gisik-nokma/ui`).
- Produces:
  - `submitChildEvent(source, event): Promise<GuardedOutcome>` — `GuardedOutcome = { kind: 'committed'; result } | { kind: 'needs-reopen'; refusal } | { kind: 'refused'; refusal } | { kind: 'invalid'; result }`
  - `reopenAndProceed(source, plan: ReopenPlan, make: MakeSubmitted): Promise<ReopenOutcome>` — `ReopenPlan = { parent: { ticketId; workspaceId }; toStateId; childTicketId; childEvent }`; `ReopenOutcome = { kind: 'committed'; parentResult; childResult } | { kind: 'parent-refused'; refusal } | { kind: 'child-refused-after-reopen'; parentResult; refusal } | { kind: 'invalid'; result }`
  - `reopenStateOptions(def: WorkflowDef, currentStateId: string): Array<{ id: string; name: string }>` — non-terminal states with a *declared* transition from the parent's current state (structural filter only; rule refusals still surface on attempt, per the owner decision)
  - `ReopenDialog` / `ReopenDialogProps`; `NewTicketDialog` / `NewTicketDialogProps`; `CreateTicketFlow` / `CreateTicketFlowProps` — the wired flow the shell mounts
- **Ordering is the contract:** on acceptance the parent's `ticket.transitioned` (with `cause: { kind: 'reopened-for-child', childTicketId }`) is ingested strictly BEFORE the child event, in two sequential calls — the parent must be non-terminal by the time the child claim is evaluated. `childTicketId` is the child's ENTITY id (for `ticket.created`, the client-generated ticket ULID), not the event id. The refused child envelope is resubmitted as-is: an interactive refusal records nothing (coordination amendment 16), so its event id is still unused.

```ts
      expect(outcome.refusal.reason).toBe('A task cannot sit under a sub-task.');
    }
  });
});
```

`packages/app-core/src/flows/guarded-child-event.ts`:

```ts
import type { IngestResult, SubmittedEvent, WorkflowDef } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import { PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE } from '../precheck.js';
import type { MakeSubmitted } from '../submit.js';

export type Refusal = { rule: string; reason: string };

export type GuardedOutcome =
  | { kind: 'committed'; result: IngestResult }
  | { kind: 'needs-reopen'; refusal: Refusal }
  | { kind: 'refused'; refusal: Refusal }
  | { kind: 'invalid'; result: IngestResult };

/**
 * Submit a child-placing claim (ticket.created with a parent, or
 * ticket.reparented). A hierarchy.parent-terminal refusal is not an error —
 * it is the moment the owner decision fires: the caller offers the reopen.
 */
export async function submitChildEvent(source: DataSource, event: SubmittedEvent): Promise<GuardedOutcome> {
  const [result] = await source.ingest([event]);
  if (result.status === 'committed') return { kind: 'committed', result };
  if (result.status === 'invalid') return { kind: 'invalid', result };
  if (result.rule === PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE) {
    return { kind: 'needs-reopen', refusal: { rule: result.rule, reason: result.reason } };
  }
  return { kind: 'refused', refusal: { rule: result.rule, reason: result.reason } };
}

/**
 * Non-terminal states the parent can STRUCTURALLY reach from its current
 * terminal state (a declared transition, from-state or '*'). Rule-based
 * refusals (restrict-to and kin) are NOT pre-filtered — the owner decision
 * says they are honoured on attempt and surfaced, not silently bypassed.
 */
export function reopenStateOptions(def: WorkflowDef, currentStateId: string): Array<{ id: string; name: string }> {
  const reachable = new Set(
    def.transitions
      .filter((transition) => transition.from === currentStateId || transition.from === '*')
      .map((transition) => transition.to),
  );
  return def.states
    .filter((state) => state.category !== 'terminal' && reachable.has(state.id))
    .map((state) => ({ id: state.id, name: state.name }));
}

export type ReopenPlan = {
  parent: { ticketId: string; workspaceId: string };
  toStateId: string;
  /** The child's ENTITY id (for ticket.created: the new ticket's ULID). */
  childTicketId: string;
  childEvent: SubmittedEvent;
};

export type ReopenOutcome =
  | { kind: 'committed'; parentResult: IngestResult; childResult: IngestResult }
  | { kind: 'parent-refused'; refusal: Refusal }
  | { kind: 'child-refused-after-reopen'; parentResult: IngestResult; refusal: Refusal }
  | { kind: 'invalid'; result: IngestResult };

/**
 * The accepted reopen: parent ticket.transitioned FIRST, carrying the cause
 * that boards render ("reopened — live work added"), THEN the child claim —
 * the same envelope that was refused (an interactive refusal records nothing,
 * so its id is unused). Sequential on purpose: the parent must be
 * non-terminal by the time the child is evaluated, and a parent refusal must
 * stop the child from ever being submitted. If the child is refused for some
 * OTHER reason after the reopen committed, the reopen stands as recorded,
 * attributed history — nothing is rolled back, nothing is hidden.
 */
export async function reopenAndProceed(
  source: DataSource,
  plan: ReopenPlan,
  make: MakeSubmitted,
): Promise<ReopenOutcome> {
  const parentTransition = make({
    workspaceId: plan.parent.workspaceId,
    entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: plan.parent.ticketId },
    type: 'ticket.transitioned',
    payload: {
      toStateId: plan.toStateId,
      cause: { kind: 'reopened-for-child', childTicketId: plan.childTicketId },
    },
  });
  const [parentResult] = await source.ingest([parentTransition]);
  if (parentResult.status === 'refused') {
    return { kind: 'parent-refused', refusal: { rule: parentResult.rule, reason: parentResult.reason } };
  }
  if (parentResult.status === 'invalid') return { kind: 'invalid', result: parentResult };

  const [childResult] = await source.ingest([plan.childEvent]);
  if (childResult.status === 'committed') return { kind: 'committed', parentResult, childResult };
  if (childResult.status === 'invalid') return { kind: 'invalid', result: childResult };
  return {
    kind: 'child-refused-after-reopen',
    parentResult,
    refusal: { rule: childResult.rule, reason: childResult.reason },
  };
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { IngestResult, SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import { CreateTicketFlow } from '../../src/views/create-ticket-flow.js';
import { createFakeDataSource } from '../helpers/fake-data-source.js';
import { PARENT, WF_ERRAND, WS, claim, ticketStateSeed } from '../helpers/seed.js';

const PARENT_READ = {
  ticket: ticketStateSeed({
    id: PARENT,
    stateId: 'done',
    stateCategory: 'terminal',
    fields: { title: { value: 'Registrations', actorId: 'user_kiro', clientTime: 'c', serverTime: 's' } },
  }),
  title: 'Registrations',
  stateProvenance: claim(),
  parent: null,
  rollup: null,
};

function committed(eventId: string): IngestResult {
  return { status: 'committed', eventId, seq: 10, serverTime: '2026-08-20T09:20:00.000Z' };
}

function flowSource(script: (call: number, events: SubmittedEvent[]) => IngestResult[]) {
  let call = 0;
  return createFakeDataSource(
    {
      getTicket: async () => PARENT_READ,
      getWorkflowDef: async () => WF_ERRAND,
    },
    (events) => {
      call += 1;
      return script(call, events);
    },
  );
}

async function createTicketUnderParent(user: ReturnType<typeof userEvent.setup>) {
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New ticket' }));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Title'), 'Renew the trade licence');
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Parent'), PARENT);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
}

function renderFlow(source: ReturnType<typeof createFakeDataSource>, onCreated = vi.fn()) {
  render(
    <CreateTicketFlow
      dataSource={source}
      workspaceId={WS}
      projectId="prj_1"
      currentUserId="user_kiro"
      typeOptions={[{ id: 'task', name: 'Task' }]}
      parentCandidates={[{ id: PARENT, title: 'Registrations' }]}
      onCreated={onCreated}
    />,
  );
  return onCreated;
}

describe('the reopen option (owner decision) — full flow, planted defects throughout', () => {
  it('offers the reopen at the moment of refusal, then emits parent-with-cause BEFORE the child, and both commit', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const source = flowSource((call, events) => {
      if (call === 1) {
        return events.map((event) => ({
          status: 'refused' as const,
          eventId: event.id,
          rule: 'hierarchy.parent-terminal',
          reason: 'Parent "Registrations" is Done; it must reopen because it is acquiring live work.',
        }));
      }
      return events.map((event) => committed(event.id));
    });
    const onCreated = renderFlow(source);
    await createTicketUnderParent(user);

    // The dialog names the parent, its terminal state, and the engine's reason verbatim.
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Reopen parent?' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent('Registrations is Done.');
    expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent(
      'Parent "Registrations" is Done; it must reopen because it is acquiring live work.',
    );

    // The state picker offers only structurally reachable non-terminal states.
    const picker = screen.getByLabelText('Reopen to');
    const optionValues = [...picker.querySelectorAll('option')].map((option) => option.getAttribute('value'));
    expect(optionValues.sort()).toEqual(['doing', 'todo']);

    await user.selectOptions(picker, 'doing');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reopen and proceed' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(source.ingested).toHaveLength(3));
    const [refusedCreate, parentBatch, childBatch] = source.ingested;
    const childTicketId = refusedCreate[0].entity.id;
    expect(parentBatch[0].type).toBe('ticket.transitioned');
    expect(parentBatch[0].entity).toEqual({ kind: 'ticket', id: PARENT });
    expect(parentBatch[0].payload).toEqual({
      toStateId: 'doing',
      cause: { kind: 'reopened-for-child', childTicketId },
    });
    expect(childBatch[0]).toBe(refusedCreate[0]); // the same claim, retried after the remedy
    expect(onCreated).toHaveBeenCalledWith(childTicketId);
  });

  it("PLANTED DEFECT: the parent's own rules are honoured, not bypassed — its refusal renders verbatim and the child is never sent", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const source = flowSource((call, events) => {
      if (call === 1) {
        return events.map((event) => ({
          status: 'refused' as const,
          eventId: event.id,
          rule: 'hierarchy.parent-terminal',
          reason: 'Parent "Registrations" is Done; it must reopen because it is acquiring live work.',
        }));
      }
      return events.map((event) => ({
        status: 'refused' as const,
        eventId: event.id,
        rule: 'restrict-to',
        reason: 'Only Anselma Marak may move this out of Done.',
      }));
    });
    renderFlow(source);
    await createTicketUnderParent(user);
    await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Reopen parent?' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reopen and proceed' }));

  });

  it('a child refusal after a committed reopen reports both truths — the reopen is recorded history', async () => {

          status: 'refused' as const,

    });
    renderFlow(source);
    await createTicketUnderParent(user);
    await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Reopen parent?' });
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Reopen to'), 'doing');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reopen and proceed' }));

    const alertBox = await screen.findByRole('alert');
    expect(alertBox).toHaveTextContent('Registrations was reopened — that is now recorded history.');
    expect(alertBox).toHaveTextContent('hierarchy.rank: A task cannot sit under a sub-task.');
  });

  it('an ordinary successful create never shows the dialog', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const source = flowSource((_call, events) => events.map((event) => committed(event.id)));
    const onCreated = renderFlow(source);
    await createTicketUnderParent(user);
    await waitFor(() => expect(onCreated).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Reopen parent?' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/reopen-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button, Dialog } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';

export type ReopenDialogProps = {
  open: boolean;
  parentTitle: string;
  parentStateName: string;
  /** The engine's refusal reason, shown verbatim — the remedy is named in it. */
  refusalReason: string;
  reopenStates: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>;
  /** The parent's own transition rules still apply; their refusal renders verbatim here. */
  parentRefusal: { rule: string; reason: string } | null;
  onAccept: (toStateId: string) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

/**
 * The owner decision, as UI: "Parent X is <terminal state>. Reopen it to
 * [non-terminal state] and proceed?" — offered at the moment the refusal
 * happens, never silently applied.
 */
export function ReopenDialog({
  open,
  parentTitle,
  parentStateName,
  refusalReason,
  reopenStates,
  parentRefusal,
  onAccept,
  onCancel,
}: ReopenDialogProps) {
  const [toStateId, setToStateId] = useState(reopenStates[0]?.id ?? '');
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} title="Reopen parent?" onClose={onCancel}>
      <p className="gn-reopen__summary">
        {parentTitle} is {parentStateName}.
      </p>
      <p className="gn-reopen__reason">{refusalReason}</p>
      <label className="gn-reopen__picker">
        Reopen to
        <select value={toStateId} onChange={(event) => setToStateId(event.target.value)}>
          {reopenStates.map((state) => (
            <option key={state.id} value={state.id}>
              {state.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>
      {parentRefusal !== null ? (
        <p role="alert" className="gn-refusal gn-refusal--server">
          Refused by the server — {parentRefusal.rule}: {parentRefusal.reason}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <div className="gn-reopen__actions">
        <Button onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>
        <Button variant="primary" disabled={toStateId === ''} onClick={() => onAccept(toStateId)}>
          Reopen and proceed
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button, Dialog } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';

export type NewTicketDialogProps = {
  open: boolean;
  typeOptions: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>;
  parentCandidates: Array<{ id: string; title: string }>;
  onCreate: (draft: { title: string; typeId: string; parentId: string | null }) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
};

export function NewTicketDialog({ open, typeOptions, parentCandidates, onCreate, onClose }: NewTicketDialogProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [typeId, setTypeId] = useState(typeOptions[0]?.id ?? '');
  const [parentId, setParentId] = useState('');
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} title="New ticket" onClose={onClose}>
      <label className="gn-new-ticket__field">
        Title
        <input value={title} onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)} />
      </label>
      <label className="gn-new-ticket__field">
        Type
        <select value={typeId} onChange={(event) => setTypeId(event.target.value)}>
          {typeOptions.map((option) => (
            <option key={option.id} value={option.id}>
              {option.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>
      <label className="gn-new-ticket__field">
        Parent
        <select value={parentId} onChange={(event) => setParentId(event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">None (project root)</option>
          {parentCandidates.map((candidate) => (
            <option key={candidate.id} value={candidate.id}>
              {candidate.title}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>
      <div className="gn-new-ticket__actions">
        <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          disabled={title.trim() === '' || typeId === ''}
          onClick={() => onCreate({ title: title.trim(), typeId, parentId: parentId === '' ? null : parentId })}
        >
          Create
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`packages/app-core/src/views/create-ticket-flow.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { newUlid, type SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
import { Button } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { ClientStateCache } from '../cache.js';
import type { DataSource } from '../data-source.js';
import {
  reopenAndProceed,
  reopenStateOptions,
  submitChildEvent,
  type Refusal,
} from '../flows/guarded-child-event.js';
import { PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE, precheckEvent } from '../precheck.js';
import { makeSubmittedFactory } from '../submit.js';
import { NewTicketDialog } from './new-ticket-dialog.js';
import { ReopenDialog } from './reopen-dialog.js';

export type CreateTicketFlowProps = {
  dataSource: DataSource;
  cache?: ClientStateCache;
  workspaceId: string;
  projectId: string;
  currentUserId: string;
  typeOptions: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>;
  parentCandidates: Array<{ id: string; title: string }>;
  onCreated: (ticketId: string) => void;
};

type ReopenPrompt = {
  childEvent: SubmittedEvent;
  childTicketId: string;
  parentId: string;
  parentTitle: string;
  parentStateName: string;
  reopenStates: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>;
  refusalReason: string;
};

/**
 * "New ticket" wired through the guarded flow: an instant pre-check preview
 * when the parent is cached, the server's refusal otherwise, and — on
 * hierarchy.parent-terminal either way — the reopen offer at the moment it
 * happens (owner decision). Nothing is retried silently; every refusal is
 * either remedied through the dialog or rendered verbatim.
 */
export function CreateTicketFlow({
  dataSource,
  cache,
  workspaceId,
  projectId,
  currentUserId,
  typeOptions,
  parentCandidates,
  onCreated,
}: CreateTicketFlowProps) {
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [reopenPrompt, setReopenPrompt] = useState<ReopenPrompt | null>(null);
  const [parentRefusal, setParentRefusal] = useState<Refusal | null>(null);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<{ headline: string | null; refusal: Refusal } | null>(null);
  const make = makeSubmittedFactory({ actorId: currentUserId, origin: 'web' });

  const openReopenPrompt = async (childEvent: SubmittedEvent, parentId: string, refusalReason: string) => {
    const [parentRead, def] = await Promise.all([
      dataSource.query.getTicket(workspaceId, parentId),
      dataSource.query
        .getTicket(workspaceId, parentId)
        .then((read) => (read === null ? null : dataSource.query.getWorkflowDef(workspaceId, read.ticket.workflowId))),
    ]);
    if (parentRead === null || def === null) {
      setFailure({ headline: null, refusal: { rule: PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE, reason: refusalReason } });
      return;
    }
    const parentStateName =
      def.states.find((state) => state.id === parentRead.ticket.stateId)?.name ?? parentRead.ticket.stateId;
    setReopenPrompt({
      childEvent,
      childTicketId: childEvent.entity.id,
      parentId,
      parentTitle: parentRead.title,
      parentStateName,
      reopenStates: reopenStateOptions(def, parentRead.ticket.stateId),
      refusalReason,
    });
  };

  const create = async (draft: { title: string; typeId: string; parentId: string | null }) => {
    const childEvent = make({
      workspaceId,
      entity: { kind: 'ticket', id: newUlid() },
      type: 'ticket.created',
      payload: { typeId: draft.typeId, projectId, parentId: draft.parentId, title: draft.title },
    });

    if (cache !== undefined && draft.parentId !== null) {
      const preview = await precheckEvent(childEvent, cache);
      if (preview.kind === 'refused' && preview.rule === PARENT_TERMINAL_RULE) {
        // The instant offer — no round trip needed when the parent is cached.
        await openReopenPrompt(childEvent, draft.parentId, preview.reason);
        return;
      }
    }

    const outcome = await submitChildEvent(dataSource, childEvent);
    if (outcome.kind === 'committed') {
      setDialogOpen(false);
      setFailure(null);
      onCreated(childEvent.entity.id);
      return;
    }
    if (outcome.kind === 'needs-reopen' && draft.parentId !== null) {
      await openReopenPrompt(childEvent, draft.parentId, outcome.refusal.reason);
      return;
    }
    if (outcome.kind === 'refused') setFailure({ headline: null, refusal: outcome.refusal });
    if (outcome.kind === 'invalid') {
      setFailure({ headline: null, refusal: { rule: outcome.result.status, reason: 'The claim was malformed.' } });
    }
  };

  const acceptReopen = async (toStateId: string) => {
    if (reopenPrompt === null) return;
    const outcome = await reopenAndProceed(
      dataSource,
      {
        parent: { ticketId: reopenPrompt.parentId, workspaceId },
        toStateId,
        childTicketId: reopenPrompt.childTicketId,
        childEvent: reopenPrompt.childEvent,
      },
      make,
    );
    if (outcome.kind === 'committed') {
      const createdId = reopenPrompt.childTicketId;
      setReopenPrompt(null);
      setDialogOpen(false);
      onCreated(createdId);
      return;
    }
    if (outcome.kind === 'parent-refused') {
      setParentRefusal(outcome.refusal); // shown inside the dialog, verbatim
      return;
    }
    if (outcome.kind === 'child-refused-after-reopen') {
      setReopenPrompt(null);
      setFailure({
        headline: `${reopenPrompt.parentTitle} was reopened — that is now recorded history.`,
        refusal: outcome.refusal,
      });
      return;
    }
    setReopenPrompt(null);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setDialogOpen(true)}>
        New ticket
      </Button>
      <NewTicketDialog
        open={dialogOpen}
        typeOptions={typeOptions}
        parentCandidates={parentCandidates}
        onCreate={(draft) => void create(draft)}
        onClose={() => setDialogOpen(false)}
      />
      {reopenPrompt !== null ? (
        <ReopenDialog
          open
          parentTitle={reopenPrompt.parentTitle}
          parentStateName={reopenPrompt.parentStateName}
          refusalReason={reopenPrompt.refusalReason}
          reopenStates={reopenPrompt.reopenStates}
          parentRefusal={parentRefusal}
          onAccept={(toStateId) => void acceptReopen(toStateId)}
          onCancel={() => {
            setReopenPrompt(null);
            setParentRefusal(null);
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
      {failure !== null ? (
        <p role="alert" className="gn-refusal gn-refusal--server">
          {failure.headline !== null ? <span>{failure.headline} </span> : null}
          {failure.headline !== null ? 'The child was then refused — ' : 'Refused by the server — '}
          {failure.refusal.rule}: {failure.refusal.reason}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `themeStylesheet` + components (`@gisik-nokma/ui`), everything exported from `@gisik-nokma/app-core` and `@gisik-nokma/app-core/online`; Plan 4's `/api/auth/*` (better-auth: `phoneNumber`, `magicLink`, `passkey` plugins, Google social provider).
- Produces: the running web client. `apps/web` routes: `/` (redirect), `/sign-in`, `/workspaces`, `/w/[workspaceId]/my-work`, `/w/[workspaceId]/p/[projectId]` (Board | List tabs, attention strip above Board, ticket sheet via `?ticket=`), `/invite/[token]` (Task 15). Dev topology: Next rewrites proxy `/v1/*`, `/api/auth/*` and `/api/workspaces/*` to the API server (same-origin cookies, no CORS); the WebSocket goes direct to the server origin (`NEXT_PUBLIC_SERVER_URL`) because Next dev does not proxy WS upgrades.

If the pinned version's client surface differs, adapt ONLY `src/auth-client.ts` and the four call sites in `app/sign-in/page.tsx`; flows and copy stay.

`apps/web/next.config.ts`:

```ts
import type { NextConfig } from 'next';

const serverOrigin = process.env.SERVER_ORIGIN ?? 'http://localhost:3000';

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  async rewrites() {
    // Same-origin proxying: the browser talks to Next; Next forwards to the
    // API server. Cookies stay first-party; no CORS. The WebSocket is the
    // exception (Next dev does not proxy upgrades) — it goes direct.
    return [
      { source: '/v1/:path*', destination: `${serverOrigin}/v1/:path*` },
      { source: '/api/auth/:path*', destination: `${serverOrigin}/api/auth/:path*` },
      { source: '/api/workspaces/:path*', destination: `${serverOrigin}/api/workspaces/:path*` },
    ];
  },
};

export default nextConfig;
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { themeStylesheet } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import '@gisik-nokma/ui/ui.css';
import './globals.css';
import { Providers } from '../src/providers.js';

export const metadata = { title: 'Gisik Nokma' };

/** Applies a stored explicit theme before first paint — no flash, and the
 * OS-preference default (from themeStylesheet) still wins when unset. */
const themeInitScript =
  "try{var stored=localStorage.getItem('gn-theme');if(stored==='light'||stored==='dark'){document.documentElement.dataset.theme=stored;}}catch(error){}";

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <script dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: themeInitScript }} />
        <style dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: themeStylesheet() }} />
      </head>
      <body>
        <Providers>{children}</Providers>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
```

```ts
'use client';

import { createAuthClient } from 'better-auth/react';
import { magicLinkClient, phoneNumberClient } from 'better-auth/client/plugins';
import { passkeyClient } from 'better-auth/client/plugins';

/** Same-origin: /api/auth/* is proxied to the server by next.config.ts. */

  baseURL: typeof window === 'undefined' ? 'http://localhost:3001' : window.location.origin,
  plugins: [phoneNumberClient(), magicLinkClient(), passkeyClient()],
});
```

`apps/web/src/providers.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { createContext, useContext, useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  createClientStateCache,
  withCacheFeed,
  type ClientStateCache,
  type DataSource,
} from '@gisik-nokma/app-core';
import { createOnlineDataSource } from '@gisik-nokma/app-core/online';
import { authClient } from './auth-client.js';

type AppServices = { dataSource: DataSource; cache: ClientStateCache };

const AppServicesContext = createContext<AppServices | null>(null);

export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const services = useMemo<AppServices>(() => {
    const cache = createClientStateCache();
    const online = createOnlineDataSource({
      baseUrl: '', // same-origin; Next rewrites proxy to the server
      streamBaseUrl: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SERVER_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000',
    });
    return { dataSource: withCacheFeed(online, cache), cache };
  }, []);
  return <AppServicesContext.Provider value={services}>{children}</AppServicesContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAppServices(): AppServices {
  const services = useContext(AppServicesContext);
  if (services === null) throw new Error('useAppServices requires <Providers> above it');
  return services;
}

/** The signed-in user, or null while the session loads / when signed out. */
export function useCurrentUser(): { id: string; name: string } | null {
  const session = authClient.useSession();
  const user = session.data?.user;
  return user === undefined ? null : { id: user.id, name: user.name ?? user.id };
}
```

`apps/web/src/theme-toggle.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';

type ThemeChoice = 'system' | 'light' | 'dark';
const ORDER: ThemeChoice[] = ['system', 'light', 'dark'];

export function ThemeToggle() {
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState<ThemeChoice>('system');

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('gn-theme');
    if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark') setChoice(stored);
  }, []);

  const apply = (next: ThemeChoice) => {
    setChoice(next);
    if (next === 'system') {
      delete document.documentElement.dataset.theme;
      localStorage.removeItem('gn-theme');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.dataset.theme = next;
      localStorage.setItem('gn-theme', next);
    }
  };

  const next = ORDER[(ORDER.indexOf(choice) + 1) % ORDER.length];
  return (
    <Button aria-label={`Theme: ${choice}. Switch to ${next}.`} onClick={() => apply(next)}>
      theme: {choice}
    </Button>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import { authClient } from './auth-client.js';
import { useCurrentUser } from './providers.js';
import { ThemeToggle } from './theme-toggle.js';

export function AppNav({ workspaceId }: { workspaceId?: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const user = useCurrentUser();
  return (
    <nav className="gn-nav">
      <Link href="/workspaces">Workspaces</Link>
      {workspaceId !== undefined ? <Link href={`/w/${workspaceId}/my-work`}>My Work</Link> : null}
      <span className="gn-nav__spacer" />
      <ThemeToggle />
      {user !== null ? (
        <Button
          onClick={() =>
            void authClient.signOut().then(() => {
              router.push('/sign-in');
            })
          }
        >
          Sign out {user.name}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ThemeToggle } from '../src/theme-toggle.js';

beforeEach(() => {
  localStorage.clear();
  delete document.documentElement.dataset.theme;
});

describe('ThemeToggle — explicit choice wins, system is the default', () => {
  it('cycles system → light → dark, stamping data-theme and persisting', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<ThemeToggle />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button')); // → light
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe('light');
    expect(localStorage.getItem('gn-theme')).toBe('light');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button')); // → dark
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe('dark');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button')); // → system
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBeUndefined();
    expect(localStorage.getItem('gn-theme')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const sendOtp = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: {}, error: null }));
const verify = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: {}, error: null }));
const magicLink = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: {}, error: null }));
const social = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: {}, error: null }));
const passkey = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: {}, error: null }));

vi.mock('../src/auth-client.js', () => ({
  authClient: {
    phoneNumber: { sendOtp, verify },
    signIn: { magicLink, social, passkey },
    useSession: () => ({ data: null }),
  },
}));

vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  useRouter: () => ({ push: vi.fn() }),
  useSearchParams: () => new URLSearchParams(),
}));

import SignInPage from '../app/sign-in/page.js';

describe('sign-in — all four passwordless methods (spec §6)', () => {
  it('offers phone OTP, magic link, Google and passkey — and nothing password-shaped', () => {
    render(<SignInPage />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Phone' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Email link' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue with Google' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in with a passkey' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText(/password/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('phone flow: request the code, then verify it', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<SignInPage />);
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Phone number'), '+919812345678');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send code' }));
    expect(sendOtp).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ phoneNumber: '+919812345678' });
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('One-time code'), '123456');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify and sign in' }));
    expect(verify).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ phoneNumber: '+919812345678', code: '123456' });
  });

  it('magic-link flow sends the email', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<SignInPage />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Email link' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'anselma@example.com');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send magic link' }));
    expect(magicLink).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: 'anselma@example.com', callbackURL: '/workspaces' });
  });

  it('Google and passkey call their sign-in methods', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<SignInPage />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue with Google' }));
    expect(social).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ provider: 'google', callbackURL: '/workspaces' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in with a passkey' }));
    expect(passkey).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button, Card } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import { authClient } from '../../src/auth-client.js';

type Method = 'phone' | 'email';

export default function SignInPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const next = searchParams.get('next') ?? '/workspaces';
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<Method>('phone');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [codeSent, setCodeSent] = useState(false);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [emailSent, setEmailSent] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fail = (message: string | undefined) => setError(message ?? 'Sign-in failed. Try again.');

  return (
    <main className="gn-shell">
      <h1 className="gn-page-title">Gisik Nokma</h1>
      <Card className="gn-sign-in">
        <div role="tablist" className="gn-view-tabs">
          <button role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={method === 'phone'} onClick={() => setMethod('phone')}>
            Phone
          </button>
          <button role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={method === 'email'} onClick={() => setMethod('email')}>
            Email link
          </button>
        </div>

        {method === 'phone' ? (
          <div className="gn-sign-in__method">
            <label>
              Phone number
              <input value={phone} onChange={(event) => setPhone(event.target.value)} placeholder="+91…" />
            </label>
            {!codeSent ? (
              <Button
                variant="primary"
                onClick={() =>
                  void authClient.phoneNumber
                    .sendOtp({ phoneNumber: phone })
                    .then(({ error: sendError }) => (sendError ? fail(sendError.message) : setCodeSent(true)))
                }
              >
                Send code
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <>
                <label>
                  One-time code
                  <input value={code} onChange={(event) => setCode(event.target.value)} />
                </label>
                <Button
                  variant="primary"
                  onClick={() =>
                    void authClient.phoneNumber
                      .verify({ phoneNumber: phone, code })
                      .then(({ error: verifyError }) => (verifyError ? fail(verifyError.message) : router.push(next)))
                  }
                >
                  Verify and sign in
                </Button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="gn-sign-in__method">
            <label>
              Email
              <input type="email" value={email} onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)} />
            </label>
            {emailSent ? (
              <p>Check your inbox — the link signs you in.</p>
            ) : (
              <Button
                variant="primary"
                onClick={() =>
                  void authClient.signIn
                    .magicLink({ email, callbackURL: next })
                    .then(({ error: linkError }) => (linkError ? fail(linkError.message) : setEmailSent(true)))
                }
              >
                Send magic link
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="gn-sign-in__alternatives">
          <Button
            onClick={() => void authClient.signIn.social({ provider: 'google', callbackURL: next }).then(({ error: socialError }) => socialError && fail(socialError.message))}
          >
            Continue with Google
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() =>
              void authClient.signIn.passkey().then((result) => {
                if (result?.error) fail(result.error.message);
                else router.push(next);
              })
            }
          >
            Sign in with a passkey
          </Button>
        </div>

        {error !== null ? (
          <p role="alert" className="gn-refusal gn-refusal--server">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Card } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import type { WorkspaceRef } from '@gisik-nokma/app-core';
import { AppNav } from '../../src/app-nav.js';
import { useAppServices, useCurrentUser } from '../../src/providers.js';

export default function WorkspacesPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { dataSource } = useAppServices();
  const user = useCurrentUser();
  const [workspaces, setWorkspaces] = useState<WorkspaceRef[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    dataSource.query.getMyWorkspaces().then(setWorkspaces, (cause: unknown) => {
      const message = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause);
      if (message.includes('401')) router.push('/sign-in');
      else setError(message);
    });
  }, [dataSource, router]);

  return (
    <main className="gn-shell">
      <AppNav />
      <h1 className="gn-page-title">Workspaces{user !== null ? ` — ${user.name}` : ''}</h1>
      {error !== null ? <p role="alert">{error}</p> : null}
      {workspaces === null ? (
        <p>Loading…</p>
      ) : workspaces.length === 0 ? (
        <p>No workspaces yet. Ask an admin for an invite link.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="gn-workspace-list">
          {workspaces.map((workspace) => (
            <li key={workspace.id}>
              <Card>
                <h2>{workspace.name}</h2>
                <ul>
                  {workspace.projects.map((project) => (
                    <li key={project.id}>
                      <Link href={`/w/${workspace.id}/p/${project.id}`}>{project.name}</Link>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
                <Link href={`/w/${workspace.id}/my-work`}>My Work</Link>
              </Card>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { AttentionStrip, MyWork, useAttention, useMyWork } from '@gisik-nokma/app-core';
import { AppNav } from '../../../../src/app-nav.js';
import { useAppServices } from '../../../../src/providers.js';

export default function MyWorkPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ workspaceId: string }>();
  const workspaceId = params.workspaceId;
  const { dataSource } = useAppServices();
  const attention = useAttention(dataSource, workspaceId);
  const myWork = useMyWork(dataSource, workspaceId);

  const openTicket = (ticketId: string) => {
    // My Work is cross-project; the sheet lives on the project page.
    const item =
      myWork.data?.assigned.find((candidate) => candidate.ticketId === ticketId) ??
      myWork.data?.inTransit.find((candidate) => candidate.ticketId === ticketId);
    if (item !== undefined) router.push(`/w/${workspaceId}/p/${item.projectId}?ticket=${ticketId}`);
  };

  return (
    <main className="gn-shell">
      <AppNav workspaceId={workspaceId} />
      <h1 className="gn-page-title">My Work</h1>
      <div className="gn-shell__main">
        <AttentionStrip items={attention.data ?? []} onOpenTicket={openTicket} />
        {myWork.data !== null ? <MyWork view={myWork.data} onOpenTicket={openTicket} /> : <p>Loading…</p>}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/app/w/[workspaceId]/p/[projectId]/page.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import {
  AttentionStrip,
  Board,
  CreateTicketFlow,
  TicketList,
  TicketSheet,
  useAttention,
  useBoard,
  useTicketList,
  type GroupLabels,
} from '@gisik-nokma/app-core';
import { AppNav } from '../../../../../src/app-nav.js';
import { useAppServices, useCurrentUser } from '../../../../../src/providers.js';

export default function ProjectPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ workspaceId: string; projectId: string }>();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const { workspaceId, projectId } = params;
  const { dataSource, cache } = useAppServices();
  const user = useCurrentUser();
  const currentUserId = user?.id ?? '';
  const [view, setView] = useState<'board' | 'list'>('board');

  const board = useBoard(dataSource, workspaceId, projectId);
  const list = useTicketList(dataSource, workspaceId, projectId, currentUserId);
  const attention = useAttention(dataSource, workspaceId);

  const openTicketId = searchParams.get('ticket');
  const openTicket = (ticketId: string) => router.push(`/w/${workspaceId}/p/${projectId}?ticket=${ticketId}`);
  const closeTicket = () => router.push(`/w/${workspaceId}/p/${projectId}`);

  const labels = useMemo<GroupLabels>(() => {
    const rows = list.rows ?? [];
    return {
      epicTitle: (epicId) => rows.find((row) => row.ticketId === epicId)?.title ?? epicId,
      typeName: (typeId) => typeId,
    };
  }, [list.rows]);

  const parentCandidates = (list.rows ?? []).map((row) => ({ id: row.ticketId, title: row.title }));

  return (
    <main className="gn-shell">
      <AppNav workspaceId={workspaceId} />
      <div className="gn-shell__main">
        <AttentionStrip items={attention.data ?? []} onOpenTicket={openTicket} />
        <div className="gn-view-tabs" role="tablist">
          <button role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={view === 'board'} onClick={() => setView('board')}>
            Board
          </button>
          <button role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={view === 'list'} onClick={() => setView('list')}>
            List
          </button>
          <CreateTicketFlow
            dataSource={dataSource}
            cache={cache}
            workspaceId={workspaceId}
            projectId={projectId}
            currentUserId={currentUserId}
            typeOptions={[
              { id: 'epic', name: 'Epic' },
              { id: 'story', name: 'Story' },
              { id: 'task', name: 'Task' },
              { id: 'sub-task', name: 'Sub-task' },
            ]}
            parentCandidates={parentCandidates}
            onCreated={openTicket}
          />
        </div>

        {view === 'board' ? (
          board.data !== null ? (
            <Board board={board.data} onOpenTicket={openTicket} />
          ) : (
            <p>{board.error ?? 'Loading…'}</p>
          )
        ) : list.rows !== null ? (
          <TicketList
            rows={list.rows}
            currentUserId={currentUserId}
            prefs={list.prefs}
            onPrefsChange={list.setPrefs}
            labels={labels}
            onOpenTicket={openTicket}
          />
        ) : (
          <p>{list.error ?? 'Loading…'}</p>
        )}
      </div>

      {openTicketId !== null ? (
        <TicketSheet
          dataSource={dataSource}
          cache={cache}
          workspaceId={workspaceId}
          ticketId={openTicketId}
          currentUserId={currentUserId}
          onClose={closeTicket}
        />
      ) : null}
    </main>
  );
}
```

(The built-in type options are the seeded ladder from Plan 3; a project-scoped ticket-type read is Plan 8's builder territory — Open question 7.)

- **Surface gap, named:** no plan currently owns invite issuance/acceptance server routes (Plan 4 owns membership events; better-auth handles identity only).
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createHttpInviteGateway } from '../../src/online/invites.js';
import type { HttpClient } from '../../src/online/http.js';

function fakeHttp(answers: Record<string, unknown>): HttpClient & { calls: string[] } {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  return {
    calls,
    get: async (path) => {
      calls.push(`GET ${path}`);
      return answers[path] ?? null;
    },
    post: async (path) => {
      calls.push(`POST ${path}`);
      return answers[path] ?? null;
    },
    put: async () => undefined,
  };
}

describe('createHttpInviteGateway', () => {
  it('reads an invite summary and answers null for a dead token', async () => {
    const http = fakeHttp({
      '/v1/invites/tok_1': { workspaceId: 'ws_1', workspaceName: 'Convenience Corner', role: 'guest' },
    });
    const gateway = createHttpInviteGateway(http);
    expect(await gateway.getInvite('tok_1')).toEqual({
      workspaceId: 'ws_1',
      workspaceName: 'Convenience Corner',
      role: 'guest',
    });
    expect(await gateway.getInvite('tok_dead')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('accepts through the accept route and hands back the workspace to enter', async () => {
    const http = fakeHttp({ '/v1/invites/tok_1/accept': { workspaceId: 'ws_1' } });
    const gateway = createHttpInviteGateway(http);
    expect(await gateway.acceptInvite('tok_1')).toEqual({ workspaceId: 'ws_1' });
    expect(http.calls).toContain('POST /v1/invites/tok_1/accept');
  });

  it('URI-encodes the token', async () => {
    const http = fakeHttp({});
    await createHttpInviteGateway(http).getInvite('a/b');
    expect(http.calls).toEqual(['GET /v1/invites/a%2Fb']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `invites.ts`; restore the export line; run**

`packages/app-core/src/online/invites.ts`:

```ts
import type { HttpClient } from './http.js';

export type InviteSummary = { workspaceId: string; workspaceName: string; role: 'admin' | 'member' | 'guest' };

/**
 * Invite links (spec §6): an admin mints a link; whoever holds it signs in
 * and accepts, and the server appends the membership.granted event. One-shot
 * onboarding — deliberately not part of DataSource (no replica serves this).
 * Route ownership is an open coordination question; this file is the ONLY
 * place that changes if the paths move.
 */
export type InviteGateway = {
  getInvite(token: string): Promise<InviteSummary | null>;
  acceptInvite(token: string): Promise<{ workspaceId: string }>;
};

export function createHttpInviteGateway(http: HttpClient): InviteGateway {
  return {
    async getInvite(token) {
      const raw = await http.get(`/v1/invites/${encodeURIComponent(token)}`);
      if (raw === null || typeof raw !== 'object') return null;
      const record = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (typeof record.workspaceId !== 'string' || typeof record.workspaceName !== 'string') return null;
      return {
        workspaceId: record.workspaceId,
        workspaceName: record.workspaceName,
        role: (record.role ?? 'member') as InviteSummary['role'],
      };
    },
    async acceptInvite(token) {
      const raw = await http.post(`/v1/invites/${encodeURIComponent(token)}/accept`, {});
      const record = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (typeof record.workspaceId !== 'string') throw new Error('invite acceptance failed');
      return { workspaceId: record.workspaceId };
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/web/app/invite/[token]/page.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button, Card } from '@gisik-nokma/ui';
import { createHttpClient, createHttpInviteGateway, type InviteSummary } from '@gisik-nokma/app-core/online';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../../../src/providers.js';

export default function InvitePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ token: string }>();
  const token = params.token;
  const user = useCurrentUser();
  const gateway = useMemo(
    () => createHttpInviteGateway(createHttpClient('', (input, init) => fetch(input, init))),
    [],
  );
  const [invite, setInvite] = useState<InviteSummary | null | 'loading'>('loading');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    gateway.getInvite(token).then(setInvite, () => setInvite(null));
  }, [gateway, token]);

  const accept = () =>
    void gateway
      .acceptInvite(token)
      .then(() => router.push('/workspaces'))
      .catch(() => setError('This invite could not be accepted. It may have expired — ask for a fresh link.'));

  return (
    <main className="gn-shell">
      <h1 className="gn-page-title">Gisik Nokma</h1>
      <Card className="gn-sign-in">
        {invite === 'loading' ? (
          <p>Checking the invite…</p>
        ) : invite === null ? (
          <p>This invite is no longer valid. Ask whoever sent it for a fresh link.</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <p>
              You are invited to join <strong>{invite.workspaceName}</strong> as {invite.role}.
            </p>
            {user === null ? (
              <p>
                <Link href={`/sign-in?next=${encodeURIComponent(`/invite/${token}`)}`}>Sign in to accept</Link> — any of
                the four sign-in methods works; you land back here.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={accept}>
                Join {invite.workspaceName}
              </Button>
            )}
            {error !== null ? (
              <p role="alert" className="gn-refusal gn-refusal--server">
                {error}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </>
        )}
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}
```

(This page is shell wiring, not an app-core view, so its direct use of the online gateway is legitimate — the amendment-10 scanner covers `packages/app-core/src/views/`, and this file lives in `apps/web`.)

`apps/web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts` — every server call is stubbed with `page.route`, so the smoke needs no API server and no database:

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

const SESSION = {
  user: { id: 'user_kiro', name: 'Kiro', email: 'kiro@example.com' },
  session: { id: 'ses_1', userId: 'user_kiro', expiresAt: '2027-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' },
};

const BOARD = {
  projectId: 'prj_1',
  groups: [
    {
      workflowId: 'wf-errand',
      workflowVersion: 1,
      workflowName: 'Errand',
      columns: [
        { stateId: 'todo', name: 'To do', category: 'open', blocked: false, cards: [] },
        {
          stateId: 'doing',
          name: 'Doing',
          category: 'active',
          blocked: false,
          cards: [
            {
              ticketId: 't_1',
              title: 'File the GST return',
              typeId: 'task',
              assignee: { id: 'user_anselma', name: 'Anselma Marak' },
              stateProvenance: {
                actorId: 'user_anselma',
                actorName: 'Anselma Marak',
                clientTime: '2026-08-19T09:58:12.000Z',
                serverTime: '2026-08-19T10:00:00.000Z',
              },
              cause: null,
              rollup: { totalChildren: 8, terminalChildren: 5, restrictedCount: 2 },
            },
          ],
        },
        { stateId: 'done', name: 'Done', category: 'terminal', blocked: false, cards: [] },
      ],
    },
  ],
};

async function stubApi(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.route('**/api/auth/**', (route) => route.fulfill({ json: SESSION }));
  await page.route('**/v1/me/workspaces', (route) =>
    route.fulfill({
      json: { workspaces: [{ id: 'ws_1', name: 'Convenience Corner', projects: [{ id: 'prj_1', name: 'Registrations' }] }] },
    }),
  );
  await page.route('**/v1/projects/prj_1/board', (route) => route.fulfill({ json: BOARD }));
  await page.route('**/v1/projects/prj_1/tickets**', (route) => route.fulfill({ json: { projectId: 'prj_1', rows: [] } }));
  await page.route('**/v1/workspaces/ws_1/attention', (route) => route.fulfill({ json: { items: [] } }));
  await page.route('**/v1/me/preferences', (route) => route.fulfill({ json: { version: 1, projects: {}, savedFilters: [] } }));
  await page.route('**/v1/invites/tok_e2e', (route) =>
    route.fulfill({ json: { workspaceId: 'ws_1', workspaceName: 'Convenience Corner', role: 'guest' } }),
  );
}

test('sign-in offers all four passwordless methods', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/sign-in');
  await expect(page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Phone' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Email link' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue with Google' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in with a passkey' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('workspace navigation reaches the board, and a card unfolds to attributed provenance', async ({ page }) => {
  await stubApi(page);
  await page.goto('/workspaces');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Registrations' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Doing' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('5 of 8 done · 2 restricted')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'who · when' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('marked Doing by Anselma Marak on 19 Aug 2026')).toBeVisible();
});

test('an invite link names the workspace and offers the join', async ({ page }) => {
  await stubApi(page);
  await page.goto('/invite/tok_e2e');
  await expect(page.getByText(/invited to join/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Convenience Corner')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Join Convenience Corner' })).toBeVisible();
});
```

```markdown
## 8. Web client core

- ⚙️ **Design tokens — the one colour origin** — primitives + semantic roles for light and dark in `packages/ui/src/tokens.ts`, self-declared contrast minimums tested in both themes, CSS variables emitted for every client; no colour literal is legal anywhere else in any client source tree, enforced by a scanner proven against planted defects (the base's token discipline, carried over). `packages/ui/src/tokens.ts`
- ✅ **Base components** — Button, Chip (state-category tones), Card, Sheet, Dialog, Table, Avatar; BEM classes, token variables only, Escape/scrim close on the modal pair, table scroll contained. `packages/ui/src/components/`
- ⚙️ **DataSource interface (amendment 10)** — `ingest` / typed `query` mirroring the server read surface / `subscribe(workspaceId, afterSeq, cb)`; views consume only this seam, enforced by a views-never-import-HTTP scanner; implemented online here, by replica in the apps plan. `packages/app-core/src/data-source.ts`
- ⚙️ **Online DataSource** — injected-fetch HTTP client, one loud mapper layer adapting wire JSON to the client contract (rollup spellings included), ingest capped at the contracted 100, WS stream per amendment 13 with capped jittered reconnect backoff and afterSeq resume from the highest seq seen. `packages/app-core/src/online/`
- ✅ **Local rule-engine pre-check** — the shared engine runs in the browser build against a drop-not-guess state cache for instant named-reason refusal previews; coverage gaps downgrade to not-evaluated; the server stays the authority and its refusals render verbatim. `packages/app-core/src/precheck.ts`
- ✅ **Board view** — columns exactly the workflow's states (empty ones included, blocked flag marked, one group per workflow), cards unfold to "marked <state> by <who> on <when>" surviving nameless claims, parent progress "n of m done · k restricted" over ALL children, reopen causes rendered, claims in transit badged. `packages/app-core/src/views/board.tsx`
- ✅ **List view (Asana-shaped)** — per-user columns chosen and ordered, nested expand/collapse hierarchy rows, "Recently assigned" pinned on top, five groupings proven to rearrange identical rows with identical counts; choices persisted per user via the preferences store, never as history. `packages/app-core/src/views/ticket-list.tsx`
- ✅ **My Work + attention strip** — assigned / claims in transit / contested sections with honest empty states; the computed strip renders each reason beside the claim it rests on and renders nothing when nothing needs attention. `packages/app-core/src/views/my-work.tsx`, `attention-strip.tsx`
- ✅ **Ticket sheet** — per-field claim attribution, restricted-parent placeholder distinct from project root, history tab rendering BOTH clocks labelled, transitions prompted by the workflow's declared rules (comment/evidence/field) with instant pre-check preview and verbatim server refusals; read-only documents through the pinned `EditorSlot` seam the editor plan fills. `packages/app-core/src/views/ticket-sheet.tsx`
- ✅ **Reopen-option flow (owner decision)** — `hierarchy.parent-terminal` surfaces "Reopen <parent> to […] and proceed?" at the moment of refusal; acceptance emits the parent transition carrying `cause: reopened-for-child` strictly before the child claim; the parent's own rules still refuse verbatim; a post-reopen child refusal reports both truths. `packages/app-core/src/flows/guarded-child-event.ts`
- ✅ **Web shell** — Next 15 App Router behind same-origin rewrites, light/dark from tokens with explicit choice outranking the OS, passwordless sign-in (phone OTP, magic link, Google, passkey), workspace/project navigation, invite-link acceptance with sign-in round-trip, desktop-responsive. `apps/web/`
```

```markdown
# 8. Web client core — test cases

Mirrors `Features.md` §8. Status: `❌` not covered · `✅` covered · `🟡` partial · `🧪` manual · `🚫` out of scope (with reason).

- 8.1 ✅ Every token role in both themes resolves to a declared primitive; every primitive is hex. (`tokens.test.ts`)
- 8.2 ✅ Every declared contrast pair meets its minimum in light AND dark — the base's case 1.1 reborn. (`tokens.test.ts`)
- 8.3 ✅ The theme stylesheet ships light default, explicit `data-theme` override, and OS-preference dark. (`tokens.test.ts`)
- 8.4 ✅ The colour scanner catches planted hex/rgb/hsl/oklch/color-mix defects before the clean-tree assertion is trusted. (`token-discipline.test.ts`)
- 8.5 ✅ No colour literal outside `packages/ui/src/tokens.ts` across all client source trees, present and future. (`token-discipline.test.ts`)
- 8.6 ✅ Base components: variants, default type=button, decorative chip dots, initials fallback that is never blank, Escape/scrim close, table cells and row clicks. (`components.test.tsx`)
- 8.7 ✅ The fake DataSource conforms to the interface, records batches, scripts refusals, fans out stream items. (`data-source.test.ts`)
- 8.8 ✅ `docToPlainText` walks paragraphs, mentions and hard breaks and never throws on unknown shapes. (`doc-text.test.ts`, guarded — may land via the editor plan)
- 8.9 ✅ The submit factory stamps actor/origin/clientTime/ULID and never stamps serverTime or seq. (`submit.test.ts`)
- 8.10 ✅ PLANTED DEFECT — provenance is never anonymous: nameless claims render the raw actor id, never blank, never "system"; unparseable dates render as claimed. (`provenance.test.ts`)
- 8.11 ✅ The pre-check cache serves upserts and direct children, latest workflow wins, and any stream event drops the touched snapshot. (`cache.test.ts`)
- 8.12 ✅ The cache-feed decorator feeds getTicket/getWorkflowDef results in and invalidates on stream events without swallowing them. (`cache.test.ts`)
- 8.13 ✅ Pre-check previews named-reason refusals from the REAL shared engine (require-comment proven), passes legal claims, downgrades missing coverage and roles-based restrict-to to not-evaluated, and previews hierarchy.parent-terminal from a cached terminal parent. (`precheck.test.ts` — also the client-build parity run Plan 3's case 5.20 deferred)
- 8.14 ✅ HTTP client: credentials included, JSON posts, 404→null, HttpError elsewhere. (`online/http.test.ts`)
- 8.15 ✅ Mappers adapt rollup spellings to the pinned viewer shape, keep both clocks, default versionless preferences, refuse malformed responses loudly, and demand index-aligned ingest results. (`online/mappers.test.ts`)
- 8.16 ✅ Online DataSource speaks the pinned paths verbatim — including Plan 4's `/api/` ticket read — serialises FilterSpec+grouping, and refuses oversized batches client-side. (`online/online-data-source.test.ts`)
- 8.17 ✅ Stream: subscribe frame on open, event/purge/caught-up delivered, ping answered silently, reconnect with exponential capped jittered backoff, resume from highest seen seq, unsubscribe stops everything, unknown frames ignored. (`online/stream.test.ts`)
- 8.18 ✅ PLANTED DEFECT — the HTTP scanner catches fetch/WebSocket/online-import defects; `src/` outside `online/` holds none: views never speak HTTP. (`no-http-in-views.test.ts`)
- 8.19 ✅ Board renders exactly the declared columns (empty included), marks blocked states, groups per workflow. (`views/board.test.tsx`)
- 8.20 ✅ PLANTED DEFECTS — board cards: provenance unfold survives nameless claims; "5 of 8 done · 2 restricted" with only six visible; reopen cause rendered; pending badge only while pending. (`views/board.test.tsx`, `views/rollup-text.test.ts`)
- 8.21 ✅ PLANTED DEFECT — every grouping (epic/status/assignee/type/flat) renders the same rows and identical counts, at model and component level; absence gets honest buckets. (`views/list-model.test.ts`, `views/ticket-list.test.tsx`)
- 8.22 ✅ List: columns chosen and ordered per user, attributed due-date claims, collapse hides only subtrees, prefs round-trip through GET/PUT preferences. (`views/ticket-list.test.tsx`)
- 8.23 ✅ My Work sections with honest empty states; attention items render kind, reason, and the claim provenance they rest on — id-attributed when nameless, absent when claimless, empty strip renders nothing. (`views/my-work.test.tsx`, `views/attention-strip.test.tsx`)
- 8.24 ✅ PLANTED DEFECTS — ticket sheet: per-field claims rendered; restricted parent is existence-without-title and distinct from project root; history shows BOTH labelled clocks under a three-day skew; the server refusal renders verbatim over a passing preview. (`views/ticket-sheet.test.tsx`, `views/history-list.test.tsx`)
- 8.25 ✅ Transition prompts come from the workflow's own rules: required comment gates the button, required fields are collected for field_set-before-transition, unavailable evidence upload is stated honestly and blocks. (`views/transition-dialog.test.tsx`)
- 8.26 ✅ PLANTED DEFECTS — the reopen flow end to end: offer at the moment of refusal with the engine's reason verbatim, structurally-reachable non-terminal picker only, parent-with-cause strictly before the child (same envelope retried), parent's own refusal verbatim with the child never sent, post-reopen child refusal reports both truths. (`flows/guarded-child-event.test.ts`, `views/reopen-flow.test.tsx`)

- 8.30 ✅ Playwright smoke: sign-in methods visible, workspace→project→board navigation, provenance unfold and restricted-count line rendered in a real browser. (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts` — requires `playwright install chromium`; not part of `turbo run test`)
- 8.31 🚫 Evidence upload inside the transition dialog — `POST /v1/attachments` and the uploader prop are the editor/attachments plan's (this build states the gap honestly and blocks the move).
- 8.32 🚫 Composing comments and rich-text editing — the `EditorSlot` default renders read-only plain text; the editor plan swaps in Tiptap behind the same pinned props.
- 8.33 🚫 Saved-filter management UI — the preferences document carries `savedFilters` and the type is pinned; building the management surface belongs with search UX in the API/realtime/search plan's client follow-up (Open question 6).
```

## Open questions

Ambiguities and cross-plan unknowns, flagged rather than silently resolved. None blocks execution; each names the assumption the plan proceeds on and the ONE file that changes if the answer differs.

1. **`IngestResult` definition site.** Coordination says the client-facing type lives in `@gisik-nokma/domain`; Plan 1 as written defines it in `apps/server/src/ingest/ingest-service.ts`. Task 5 hoists it into domain and re-exports from the service (one definition). If reconciliation resolves this differently, only Task 5's guard changes.
2. **Invite server routes are unowned.** Spec §6 requires invite links; no plan owns issuance/acceptance endpoints (Plan 4 owns membership events; better-auth is identity only). This plan codes the client against `GET /v1/invites/:token` and `POST /v1/invites/:token/accept` → `{workspaceId}` behind `InviteGateway`, with acceptance appending `membership.granted` server-side. Recommend a coordination amendment assigning the routes (and the invite-mint admin UI) — most naturally to Plan 4 as a follow-up or Plan 11's bootstrap. Only `invites.ts` changes if paths move; who the `membership.granted` event's actor should be (the inviting admin vs. the acceptor) is part of the same amendment.
3. **Workflow-def read endpoint.** The sheet's transition options, the reopen state picker, and the pre-check all need `WorkflowDef`s; no read route is pinned (Plan 2 built `proj_workflows` with no route; Plan 5's pinned surface omits it; Plan 8's builder will need it too). Assumed: `GET /v1/workspaces/:workspaceId/workflows/:workflowId?version=`. Only the `getWorkflowDef` adapter function changes.
4. **Workspace directory and member reads.** Navigation needs `GET /v1/me/workspaces`; name resolution needs `GET /v1/workspaces/:id/members` (display names live in better-auth's user table, so Plan 4/5 must expose them — proj_memberships alone has no names). Both are in `DataSource.query` because Plan 10's replica must serve them offline. Only the two adapter functions change.
5. **Plan 5 response JSON shapes.** Board/tickets/me-work/attention/search wire spellings are Plan 5's to finalise; the mappers are the single adaptation layer and `mapRollup` already tolerates both known rollup spellings. If Plan 5 embeds actor display names differently (or not at all), `mapProvenance` adapts; rendering already survives nameless claims by falling back to ids.
6. **Preferences document schema.** Amendment 14 pins the store and routes but not the JSON. This plan versions the client document (`{version: 1, projects, savedFilters}`) and treats anything else as empty-with-defaults. If Plan 5 pins a server schema, `mapPreferences` converts. The saved-filter *management UI* is explicitly not built here (case 8.33).
7. **Ticket-type options in the create dialog.** The shell seeds the built-in ladder (epic/story/task/sub-task, Plan 3's seeds). Custom types need a type-read endpoint (`proj_ticket_types` exists; no route pinned) — expected to land with Plan 8's builder; the `typeOptions` prop is already plumbed.
8. **better-auth client plugin surface.** Plan 4 flagged that option names follow better-auth 1.x. The same caveat applies to the React client (`phoneNumber.sendOtp/verify`, `signIn.magicLink/social/passkey`, plugin import paths). Pin the web `better-auth` version to the server's; adapt only `src/auth-client.ts` and the sign-in call sites.
9. **WebSocket origin in dev.** Next rewrites give same-origin HTTP (first-party cookies, no CORS), but Next dev does not proxy WS upgrades, so the stream connects directly to `NEXT_PUBLIC_SERVER_URL`. Browsers do not scope cookies by port, so the session cookie rides along on localhost; production should terminate both behind one origin (Plan 11's deployment shape decides).
10. **Board response granularity.** `BoardView` groups by workflow because a project can mix ticket types on different workflows and merged columns would invent states (spec §7). If Plan 5's board read returns a flat single-workflow shape instead, `mapBoardView` wraps it into one group — views unchanged.
11. **UTC-labelled timestamps.** v1 renders all times in UTC with the zone named (deterministic, honest). Viewer-local display with the zone still named is future polish — it touches only `provenance.ts`.
12. **Guarded domain edits and parallel Plan 7.** Tasks 5's guards (`docToPlainText`, `blocked`, `cause`) are check-first-skip-if-present, so either execution order of Plans 6/7 works; the orchestrator could also pre-land the domain pieces to remove the race entirely.
